```
1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Test the claim_next_task edge function to see what it returns.
     4→This script will call the edge function in both dry_run mode (to check available tasks)
     5→and actual claim mode (to see what task gets returned).
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import os
     9→import json
    10→import argparse
    11→import asyncio
    12→import aiohttp
    13→
    14→from gpu_orchestrator.database import DatabaseClient
    15→from dotenv import load_dotenv
    16→
    17→async def run_claim_next_task(worker_id: str = "test-worker-001", dry_run: bool = False):
    18→    """
    19→    Test the claim_next_task edge function.
    20→    
    21→    Args:
    22→        worker_id: Worker ID to use for claiming tasks
    23→        dry_run: If True, only check available tasks without claiming
    24→    """
    25→    
    26→    supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL")
    27→    supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
    28→    
    29→    if not supabase_url or not supabase_key:
    30→        print("❌ Missing Supabase environment configuration")
    31→        print("   Make sure SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY are set")
    32→        return None
    33→    
    34→    edge_function_url = f"{supabase_url}/functions/v1/claim-next-task"
    35→    
    36→    headers = {
    37→        "Authorization": f"Bearer {supabase_key}",
    38→        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    39→    }
    40→    
    41→    payload = {
    42→        "worker_id": worker_id,
    43→        "dry_run": dry_run
    44→    }
    45→    
    46→    print("🚀 Testing claim_next_task edge function...")
    47→    print(f"   URL: {edge_function_url}")
    48→    print(f"   Worker ID: {worker_id}")
    49→    print(f"   Dry run: {dry_run}")
    50→    print()
    51→    
    52→    try:
    53→        async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
    54→            async with session.post(edge_function_url, headers=headers, json=payload) as response:
    55→                print(f"📊 Response status: {response.status}")
    56→                
    57→                if response.status == 200:
    58→                    data = await response.json()
    59→                    print("✅ Response data:")
    60→                    print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
    61→                    
    62→                    # Extract key information
    63→                    if dry_run:
    64→                        available_tasks = data.get("available_tasks", 0)
    65→                        print(f"\n📈 Available tasks: {available_tasks}")
    66→                        return available_tasks
    67→                    else:
    68→                        # For actual claiming, the edge function returns the task data directly
    69→                        if data.get("task_id"):
    70→                            task_id = data.get("task_id")
    71→                            task_type = data.get("task_type")
    72→                            print(f"\n🎯 Claimed task: {task_id}")
    73→                            print(f"   Type: {task_type}")
    74→                            return data
    75→                        else:
    76→                            print("\n❌ No task was claimed (no available tasks)")
    77→                            return None
    78→                else:
    79→                    error_text = await response.text()
    80→                    print(f"❌ Error response: {error_text}")
    81→                    return None
    82→                    
    83→    except Exception as e:
    84→        print(f"❌ Exception calling edge function: {e}")
    85→        return None
    86→
    87→async def run_available_tasks_count():
    88→    """Test the existing count_available_tasks_via_edge_function method."""
    89→    print("🔍 Testing available tasks count via DatabaseClient...")
    90→    
    91→    try:
    92→        db = DatabaseClient()
    93→        
    94→        # Test with include_active=True (default)
    95→        count_with_active = await db.count_available_tasks_via_edge_function(include_active=True)
    96→        print(f"📊 Available tasks (including active): {count_with_active}")
    97→        
    98→        # Test with include_active=False (only queued)
    99→        count_queued_only = await db.count_available_tasks_via_edge_function(include_active=False)
   100→        print(f"📊 Available tasks (queued only): {count_queued_only}")
   101→        
   102→        return count_with_active, count_queued_only
   103→        
   104→    except Exception as e:
   105→        print(f"❌ Error testing DatabaseClient method: {e}")
   106→        return None, None
   107→
   108→async def show_task_queue_status():
   109→    """Show current task queue status from database."""
   110→    print("📋 Current task queue status...")
   111→    
   112→    try:
   113→        db = DatabaseClient()
   114→        
   115→        # Get task counts by status
   116→        result = db.supabase.table('tasks').select('status').execute()
   117→        
   118→        if result.data:
   119→            status_counts = {}
   120→            for task in result.data:
   121→                status = task['status']
   122→                status_counts[status] = status_counts.get(status, 0) + 1
   123→            
   124→            print("   Task counts by status:")
   125→            for status, count in sorted(status_counts.items()):
   126→                print(f"     {status}: {count}")
   127→        else:
   128→            print("   No tasks found in database")
   129→            
   130→        # Get recent tasks
   131→        recent_result = db.supabase.table('tasks').select('id, task_type, status, created_at, worker_id').order('created_at', desc=True).limit(5).execute()
   132→        
   133→        if recent_result.data:
   134→            print("\n   Recent tasks (last 5):")
   135→            for task in recent_result.data:
   136→                created_at = task.get('created_at', 'unknown')
   137→                worker_id = task.get('worker_id', 'none')
   138→                print(f"     {task['id'][:8]}... | {task['status']} | {task.get('task_type', 'unknown')} | worker: {worker_id} | {created_at}")
   139→        
   140→    except Exception as e:
   141→        print(f"❌ Error getting task queue status: {e}")
   142→
   143→async def main():
   144→    load_dotenv()
   145→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Test the claim_next_task edge function")
   146→    parser.add_argument('--worker-id', '-w', default='test-worker-001', help='Worker ID to use (default: test-worker-001)')
   147→    parser.add_argument('--dry-run', '-d', action='store_true', help='Only check available tasks without claiming')
   148→    parser.add_argument('--claim', '-c', action='store_true', help='Actually claim a task (default behavior)')
   149→    parser.add_argument('--status-only', '-s', action='store_true', help='Only show task queue status')
   150→    
   151→    args = parser.parse_args()
   152→    
   153→    print("=" * 60)
   154→    print("🧪 CLAIM_NEXT_TASK EDGE FUNCTION TEST")
   155→    print("=" * 60)
   156→    print()
   157→    
   158→    # Show current queue status
   159→    await show_task_queue_status()
   160→    print()
   161→    
   162→    if args.status_only:
   163→        return
   164→    
   165→    # Test the DatabaseClient method first
   166→    await run_available_tasks_count()
   167→    print()
   168→    
   169→    # Test the edge function directly
   170→    if args.dry_run:
   171→        print("🔍 Testing dry run (check available tasks)...")
   172→        result = await run_claim_next_task(args.worker_id, dry_run=True)
   173→    else:
   174→        print("🎯 Testing actual task claiming...")
   175→        result = await run_claim_next_task(args.worker_id, dry_run=False)
   176→    
   177→    print()
   178→    print("=" * 60)
   179→    print("✅ Test completed!")
   180→    
   181→    if result is not None:
   182→        if isinstance(result, int):
   183→            print(f"🔢 Number returned: {result}")
   184→        else:
   185→            print(f"📦 Object returned: {type(result).__name__}")
   186→    else:
   187→        print("❌ No result returned")
   188→
   189→if __name__ == "__main__":
   190→    asyncio.run(main())
   191→
```

```
1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Create test tasks in Supabase for testing the orchestrator.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import os
     7→import sys
     8→import json
     9→import argparse
    10→from datetime import datetime, timezone
    11→from pathlib import Path
    12→
    13→# Add project root to path
    14→sys.path.append(str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))
    15→
    16→from gpu_orchestrator.database import DatabaseClient
    17→
    18→async def create_test_tasks(count: int = 1, task_type: str = "test", project_id: str = None):
    19→    """Create test tasks in the database."""
    20→    db = DatabaseClient()
    21→    
    22→    # If no project_id provided, try to get one from existing tasks
    23→    if not project_id:
    24→        try:
    25→            result = db.supabase.table('tasks').select('project_id').limit(1).execute()
    26→            if result.data and len(result.data) > 0:
    27→                project_id = result.data[0]['project_id']
    28→                print(f"📝 Using project_id from existing task: {project_id}")
    29→            else:
    30→                print("❌ No project_id provided and no existing tasks found")
    31→                print("   Please provide a project_id using --project-id flag")
    32→                return []
    33→        except Exception as e:
    34→            print(f"❌ Error getting project_id: {e}")
    35→            return []
    36→    
    37→    print(f"🚀 Creating {count} test task(s) of type '{task_type}'...")
    38→    
    39→    created_tasks = []
    40→    for i in range(count):
    41→        try:
    42→            task_data = {
    43→                'task_type': task_type,
    44→                'project_id': project_id,
    45→                'params': {
    46→                    'test_number': i + 1,
    47→                    'processing_time': 30,  # seconds
    48→                    'description': f'Test task {i + 1} created at {datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}'
    49→                },
    50→                'status': 'Queued',
    51→                'created_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    52→            }
    53→            
    54→            result = db.supabase.table('tasks').insert(task_data).execute()
    55→            
    56→            if result.data:
    57→                task_id = result.data[0]['id']
    58→                created_tasks.append(task_id)
    59→                print(f"✅ Created task {task_id}")
    60→            else:
    61→                print(f"❌ Failed to create task {i + 1}")
    62→                
    63→        except Exception as e:
    64→            print(f"❌ Error creating task {i + 1}: {e}")
    65→    
    66→    if created_tasks:
    67→        print(f"\n🎉 Successfully created {len(created_tasks)} task(s)")
    68→        print("\nCreated task IDs:")
    69→        for task_id in created_tasks:
    70→            print(f"  - {task_id}")
    71→    else:
    72→        print("\n❌ No tasks were created")
    73→    
    74→    return created_tasks
    75→
    76→async def main():
    77→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Create test tasks for the orchestrator")
    78→    parser.add_argument('--count', '-n', type=int, default=1, help='Number of tasks to create (default: 1)')
    79→    parser.add_argument('--type', '-t', default='test', help='Task type (default: test)')
    80→    parser.add_argument('--project-id', '-p', help='Project ID to associate tasks with (optional)')
    81→    
    82→    args = parser.parse_args()
    83→    
    84→    await create_test_tasks(args.count, args.type, args.project_id)
    85→
    86→if __name__ == "__main__":
    87→    import asyncio
    88→    asyncio.run(main())
```

Excellent! Now I have comprehensive information. Let me create a final summary document based on my findings:

## Summary: Task Storage and Querying in the Headless WGP Orchestrator

Based on my comprehensive search of the codebase, here's what I found about the task system:

### 1. **Where Tasks Are Stored: Supabase PostgreSQL Database**

Tasks are stored in **Supabase** (a PostgreSQL-based service) in the **`tasks`** table. The database client is initialized using environment variables:

```python
# From: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/api_orchestrator/database.py
# and: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py

supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL")
supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
self.supabase = create_client(supabase_url, supabase_key)
```

**Environment Variables Needed:**
- `SUPABASE_URL` - The Supabase instance URL
- `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` - Service role key for authentication

---

### 2. **How to Query a Specific Task by ID**

To retrieve a task by its ID (like `ba8797d7-7304-428b-be32-370fdd529a3f`), use this pattern:

```python
# From: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/scripts/debug/services/tasks.py
result = db.supabase.table("tasks").select("*").eq("id", task_id).execute()
state = result.data[0] if result.data else None
```

**Python Example Code:**
```python
from supabase import create_client
import os

supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL")
supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
db = create_client(supabase_url, supabase_key)

task_id = "ba8797d7-7304-428b-be32-370fdd529a3f"
result = db.table("tasks").select("*").eq("id", task_id).execute()

if result.data:
    task = result.data[0]
    print(f"Task ID: {task['id']}")
    print(f"Status: {task['status']}")
    print(f"Task Type: {task['task_type']}")
    print(f"Error Message: {task.get('error_message')}")
    print(f"Worker ID: {task.get('worker_id')}")
else:
    print("Task not found")
```

---

### 3. **Task Fields and Status/Error Details**

The task record in the database contains:

**Key Fields:**
- `id` - UUID task identifier
- `status` - Current status (Queued, In Progress, Completed, Failed)
- `task_type` - Type of task (e.g., "qwen_image_edit", "fal_upscale", etc.)
- `error_message` - Error details if task failed
- `worker_id` - GPU worker that claimed the task (NULL for API tasks)
- `params` - Task parameters (JSON)
- `result_data` - Metadata and results (JSON)
- `created_at` - When task was created
- `generation_started_at` - When processing started
- `generation_processed_at` - When processing completed
- `updated_at` - Last update timestamp
- `attempts` - Number of retry attempts (0-3)

---

### 4. **Task Query Methods Available**

The codebase provides several ways to query tasks:

**A. Get Task by ID:**
```python
db.supabase.table("tasks").select("*").eq("id", task_id).execute()
```

**B. Get Tasks by Status:**
```python
db.supabase.table("tasks").select("*").eq("status", "Failed").execute()
```

**C. Get Tasks by Worker:**
```python
db.supabase.table("tasks").select("*").eq("worker_id", worker_id).execute()
```

**D. Get Recent Tasks with Filters:**
From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/scripts/debug/services/tasks.py`:

```python
query = db.supabase.table("tasks").select("*")
if status:
    query = query.eq("status", status)
if task_type:
    query = query.eq("task_type", task_type)
if worker_id:
    query = query.eq("worker_id", worker_id)
if hours:
    cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)
    query = query.gte("created_at", cutoff.isoformat())
tasks = query.order("created_at", desc=True).limit(50).execute().data
```

---

### 5. **Log Files and Centralized Logging**

Logs are stored in **two locations**:

**A. Local Log File:**
- Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/orchestrator.log`
- Contains JSON-formatted logs from orchestrator components
- Fields: asctime, name, levelname, message

**B. Centralized Supabase `system_logs` Table:**
From `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/orchestrator_common/database_log_handler.py`:

```python
# Query logs by task ID
result = db.supabase.table("system_logs") \
    .select("*") \
    .eq("task_id", task_id) \
    .order("timestamp", desc=False) \
    .execute()
```

**System Logs Query Pattern:**
```python
# From: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/scripts/debug/client.py

query = self.supabase.table("system_logs").select("*")
query = query.gte("timestamp", start_time.isoformat())
query = query.lte("timestamp", end_time.isoformat())

if task_id:
    query = query.eq("task_id", task_id)
if worker_id:
    query = query.eq("worker_id", worker_id)
if log_level:
    query = query.eq("log_level", log_level)

logs = query.order("timestamp", desc=False).limit(1000).execute().data
```

**Log Fields:**
- `timestamp` - When log was created
- `task_id` - Associated task ID (if applicable)
- `worker_id` - Associated worker ID (if applicable)
- `source_type` - Type of source (orchestrator_gpu, orchestrator_api, worker)
- `source_id` - ID of the source instance
- `log_level` - Log level (INFO, ERROR, WARNING, etc.)
- `message` - Log message
- `cycle_number` - Orchestrator cycle number (if applicable)

---

### 6. **Available Helper Classes/Scripts**

**Debug CLI Tool:**
Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/scripts/debug/client.py`

Usage:
```python
from scripts.debug.client import DebugClient

client = DebugClient()

# Get complete task info (DB state + logs)
task_info = client.get_task_info("ba8797d7-7304-428b-be32-370fdd529a3f")
print(f"Task State: {task_info.state}")
print(f"Logs: {task_info.logs}")

# Get recent tasks
tasks_summary = client.get_recent_tasks(
    limit=50,
    status="Failed",
    hours=24
)

# Get task timeline (logs only)
from scripts.debug.client import LogQueryClient
log_client = LogQueryClient()
timeline = log_client.get_task_timeline("ba8797d7-7304-428b-be32-370fdd529a3f")
```

**Task Utilities:**
Path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/api_orchestrator/task_utils.py`

Functions:
- `claim_next_task()` - Claims next available task
- `mark_complete_via_edge_function()` - Marks task as complete
- `mark_failed_via_edge_function()` - Marks task as failed
- `update_task_metadata()` - Updates task result_data field
- `get_task_metadata()` - Retrieves task metadata

### 7. **Task ID Not Found in Codebase**

The specific task ID `ba8797d7-7304-428b-be32-370fdd529a3f` was **not found** in:
- Any Python source files
- The orchestrator.log file
- Documentation files
- Test files

This indicates the task either:
- Hasn't been processed by this orchestrator yet
- Was created in a different environment
- May exist in the Supabase database but hasn't generated logs

**To check if this task exists in your database, run:**

```python
from gpu_orchestrator.database import DatabaseClient

db = DatabaseClient()
result = db.supabase.table("tasks").select("*").eq("id", "ba8797d7-7304-428b-be32-370fdd529a3f").execute()

if result.data:
    print("Task found!")
    print(result.data[0])
else:
    print("Task not found in database")
```

```
1→"""
     2→Storage utilities for the API orchestrator.
     3→Handles file downloads, uploads, and storage operations with Supabase.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import os
     7→import logging
     8→import base64
     9→import mimetypes
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import Any, Dict
    12→from urllib.parse import urlparse
    13→
    14→import httpx
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→async def download_url_content(client: httpx.AsyncClient, url: str) -> bytes:
    20→    """Download content from a URL and return as bytes."""
    21→    try:
    22→        logger.info(f"Downloading content from: {url}")
    23→        response = await client.get(url, timeout=60)
    24→        response.raise_for_status()
    25→        
    26→        content = response.content
    27→        logger.info(f"Downloaded {len(content)} bytes from {url}")
    28→        return content
    29→        
    30→    except Exception as e:
    31→        logger.error(f"Failed to download from {url}: {e}")
    32→        raise
    33→
    34→
    35→async def upload_to_supabase_storage_only(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, file_data: bytes, filename: str) -> str:
    36→    """
    37→    Upload file data to Supabase storage WITHOUT marking task complete.
    38→    Returns the public URL of the uploaded file.
    39→    Used for intermediate files that need to be uploaded but task is not yet complete.
    40→    """
    41→    try:
    42→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "").rstrip("/")
    43→        
    44→        if not supabase_url:
    45→            raise Exception("SUPABASE_URL not configured")
    46→            
    47→        auth_token = os.getenv('SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN') or os.getenv('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')
    48→        
    49→        # Sanitize filename
    50→        safe_filename = "".join(c for c in filename if c.isalnum() or c in "._-").strip()
    51→        if not safe_filename:
    52→            safe_filename = f"task_{task_id}.bin"
    53→        
    54→        # Guess content type from filename
    55→        content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(safe_filename)[0] or 'application/octet-stream'
    56→        
    57→        file_size_mb = len(file_data) / (1024 * 1024)
    58→        logger.info(f"Uploading {len(file_data)} bytes ({file_size_mb:.2f}MB) to Supabase storage (no task completion) as {safe_filename}")
    59→        
    60→        # Construct storage path
    61→        storage_path = f"task_outputs/{safe_filename}"
    62→        upload_url = f"{supabase_url}/storage/v1/object/image_uploads/{storage_path}"
    63→        
    64→        headers = {
    65→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {auth_token}",
    66→            "Content-Type": content_type,
    67→            "x-upsert": "true"
    68→        }
    69→        
    70→        # Upload directly to storage
    71→        logger.info(f"Uploading directly to storage path: {storage_path}")
    72→        response = await client.post(upload_url, content=file_data, headers=headers, timeout=120)
    73→        
    74→        if response.status_code not in (200, 201):
    75→            error_text = response.text
    76→            logger.error(f"Storage upload failed with status {response.status_code}: {error_text}")
    77→            response.raise_for_status()
    78→        
    79→        # Construct public URL
    80→        public_url = f"{supabase_url}/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/{storage_path}"
    81→        
    82→        logger.info(f"Upload successful (no task completion): {public_url}")
    83→        return public_url
    84→        
    85→    except Exception as e:
    86→        logger.error(f"Failed to upload to Supabase storage: {e}")
    87→        raise
    88→
    89→
    90→async def upload_to_supabase_storage(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, file_data: bytes, filename: str, first_frame_data: str = None) -> str:
    91→    """
    92→    Upload file data to Supabase storage.
    93→    Uses optimized path based on file size:
    94→    - Files <2MB: Direct base64 upload to complete_task (fewer round trips)
    95→    - Files >=2MB: Pre-signed URL upload (no base64 overhead, no Edge Function limits)
    96→    Returns the public URL of the uploaded file.
    97→    """
    98→    try:
    99→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "").rstrip("/")
   100→        complete_task_url = f"{supabase_url}/functions/v1/complete_task"
   101→        
   102→        if not supabase_url:
   103→            raise Exception("SUPABASE_URL not configured")
   104→            
   105→        auth_token = os.getenv('SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN') or os.getenv('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')
   106→        headers = {
   107→            "Content-Type": "application/json",
   108→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {auth_token}"
   109→        }
   110→        
   111→        # Sanitize filename
   112→        safe_filename = "".join(c for c in filename if c.isalnum() or c in "._-").strip()
   113→        if not safe_filename:
   114→            # Fallback filename based on task_id and content type
   115→            parsed_url = urlparse(filename) if filename else None
   116→            ext = Path(parsed_url.path).suffix if parsed_url else ".bin"
   117→            safe_filename = f"task_{task_id}{ext}"
   118→        
   119→        # Guess content type from filename
   120→        content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(safe_filename)[0] or 'application/octet-stream'
   121→        
   122→        file_size_mb = len(file_data) / (1024 * 1024)
   123→        logger.info(f"Uploading {len(file_data)} bytes ({file_size_mb:.2f}MB) to Supabase storage as {safe_filename} (type: {content_type})")
   124→        
   125→        # Use optimized path based on file size
   126→        # Small files (<2MB): Use direct base64 upload (fewer round trips, faster for small files)
   127→        # Large files (>=2MB): Use pre-signed URLs (no base64 overhead, no Edge Function limits)
   128→        SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB = 2.0
   129→        
   130→        if file_size_mb < SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB:
   131→            # OLD PATH: Direct base64 upload to complete_task (optimal for small files)
   132→            logger.info(f"Using direct upload path for {file_size_mb:.2f}MB file (< {SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB}MB threshold)")
   133→            return await _upload_direct_base64(client, task_id, file_data, safe_filename, first_frame_data, headers, complete_task_url)
   134→        else:
   135→            # NEW PATH: Pre-signed URL upload (optimal for large files)
   136→            logger.info(f"Using pre-signed URL path for {file_size_mb:.2f}MB file (>= {SIZE_THRESHOLD_MB}MB threshold)")
   137→            generate_upload_url = f"{supabase_url}/functions/v1/generate-upload-url"
   138→            return await _upload_presigned_url(client, task_id, file_data, safe_filename, content_type, first_frame_data, headers, generate_upload_url, complete_task_url)
   139→            
   140→    except Exception as e:
   141→        logger.error(f"Failed to upload to Supabase storage: {e}")
   142→        raise
   143→
   144→
   145→async def _upload_direct_base64(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, file_data: bytes, filename: str, first_frame_data: str, headers: Dict[str, str], complete_task_url: str) -> str:
   146→    """
   147→    Upload file using direct base64 encoding to complete_task Edge Function.
   148→    Optimal for files <2MB (fewer round trips despite base64 overhead).
   149→    """
   150→    # Encode file data as base64
   151→    file_base64 = base64.b64encode(file_data).decode('utf-8')
   152→    
   153→    payload = {
   154→        "task_id": task_id,
   155→        "file_data": file_base64,
   156→        "filename": filename
   157→    }
   158→    
   159→    # Include first frame data if provided
   160→    if first_frame_data:
   161→        payload["first_frame_data"] = first_frame_data
   162→        # Generate screenshot filename based on main filename
   163→        base_name = Path(filename).stem
   164→        payload["first_frame_filename"] = f"{base_name}_screenshot.png"
   165→    
   166→    logger.info(f"Direct upload: {len(file_data)} bytes -> {len(file_base64)} chars base64")
   167→    
   168→    response = await client.post(complete_task_url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=120)
   169→    
   170→    if response.status_code != 200:
   171→        response_text = response.text
   172→        logger.error(f"Direct upload failed with status {response.status_code}: {response_text}")
   173→        
   174→    response.raise_for_status()
   175→    
   176→    result = response.json()
   177→    
   178→    # Extract the public URL from the response
   179→    public_url = result.get("public_url") or result.get("output_location")
   180→    
   181→    if not public_url:
   182→        raise Exception(f"No public URL returned from upload: {result}")
   183→        
   184→    logger.info(f"Direct upload successful: {public_url}")
   185→    return public_url
   186→
   187→
   188→async def _upload_presigned_url(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, file_data: bytes, filename: str, content_type: str, first_frame_data: str, headers: Dict[str, str], generate_upload_url: str, complete_task_url: str) -> str:
   189→    """
   190→    Upload file using pre-signed URLs.
   191→    Optimal for files >=2MB (no base64 overhead, bypasses Edge Function limits).
   192→    """
   193→    # Step 1: Generate pre-signed upload URL
   194→    generate_payload = {
   195→        "task_id": task_id,
   196→        "filename": filename,
   197→        "content_type": content_type,
   198→        "generate_thumbnail_url": bool(first_frame_data)  # Request thumbnail URL if we have screenshot
   199→    }
   200→    
   201→    logger.info(f"Requesting pre-signed upload URL for task {task_id}")
   202→    response = await client.post(generate_upload_url, headers=headers, json=generate_payload, timeout=30)
   203→    response.raise_for_status()
   204→    
   205→    upload_urls = response.json()
   206→    main_upload_url = upload_urls.get("upload_url")
   207→    storage_path = upload_urls.get("storage_path")
   208→    thumbnail_upload_url = upload_urls.get("thumbnail_upload_url")
   209→    thumbnail_storage_path = upload_urls.get("thumbnail_storage_path")
   210→    
   211→    if not main_upload_url or not storage_path:
   212→        raise Exception(f"Invalid response from generate-upload-url: {upload_urls}")
   213→    
   214→    logger.info(f"Generated upload URL for storage path: {storage_path}")
   215→    
   216→    # Step 2: Upload main file directly to storage (binary upload, no base64!)
   217→    upload_headers = {
   218→        "Content-Type": content_type,
   219→        "x-upsert": "true"  # Allow overwriting if file exists
   220→    }
   221→    
   222→    logger.info(f"Uploading {len(file_data)} bytes directly to storage...")
   223→    upload_response = await client.put(main_upload_url, content=file_data, headers=upload_headers, timeout=120)
   224→    
   225→    if upload_response.status_code not in (200, 201):
   226→        error_text = upload_response.text
   227→        logger.error(f"Direct storage upload failed with status {upload_response.status_code}: {error_text}")
   228→        upload_response.raise_for_status()
   229→    
   230→    logger.info(f"Successfully uploaded main file to storage")
   231→    
   232→    # Step 3: Upload thumbnail if provided
   233→    if first_frame_data and thumbnail_upload_url and thumbnail_storage_path:
   234→        try:
   235→            # Decode base64 screenshot data
   236→            screenshot_bytes = base64.b64decode(first_frame_data)
   237→            
   238→            thumbnail_headers = {
   239→                "Content-Type": "image/png",
   240→                "x-upsert": "true"
   241→            }
   242→            
   243→            logger.info(f"Uploading {len(screenshot_bytes)} byte thumbnail to storage...")
   244→            thumb_response = await client.put(thumbnail_upload_url, content=screenshot_bytes, headers=thumbnail_headers, timeout=60)
   245→            
   246→            if thumb_response.status_code not in (200, 201):
   247→                logger.warning(f"Thumbnail upload failed with status {thumb_response.status_code}: {thumb_response.text}")
   248→            else:
   249→                logger.info(f"Successfully uploaded thumbnail to {thumbnail_storage_path}")
   250→        except Exception as thumb_error:
   251→            # Don't fail the whole upload if thumbnail fails
   252→            logger.warning(f"Thumbnail upload failed (non-fatal): {thumb_error}")
   253→    
   254→    # Step 4: Mark task complete with storage path
   255→    complete_payload = {
   256→        "task_id": task_id,
   257→        "storage_path": storage_path
   258→    }
   259→    
   260→    # Include thumbnail path if uploaded
   261→    if first_frame_data and thumbnail_storage_path:
   262→        complete_payload["thumbnail_storage_path"] = thumbnail_storage_path
   263→    
   264→    logger.info(f"Marking task {task_id} complete with storage_path: {storage_path}")
   265→    complete_response = await client.post(complete_task_url, headers=headers, json=complete_payload, timeout=30)
   266→    
   267→    if complete_response.status_code != 200:
   268→        error_text = complete_response.text
   269→        logger.error(f"Complete task failed with status {complete_response.status_code}: {error_text}")
   270→        complete_response.raise_for_status()
   271→    
   272→    result = complete_response.json()
   273→    
   274→    # Extract the public URL from the response
   275→    public_url = result.get("public_url") or result.get("output_location")
   276→    
   277→    if not public_url:
   278→        raise Exception(f"No public URL returned from complete_task: {result}")
   279→        
   280→    logger.info(f"Pre-signed URL upload successful: {public_url}")
   281→    return public_url
   282→
   283→
   284→async def download_and_upload_to_supabase(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, external_url: str, extract_screenshot: bool = True) -> Dict[str, str]:
   285→    """
   286→    Download content from external URL and re-upload to Supabase storage.
   287→    If the content is a video and extract_screenshot=True, also extracts and uploads first frame.
   288→    Returns dict with 'url' and optionally 'screenshot_url'.
   289→    """
   290→    from .video_utils import is_video_file, extract_first_frame_bytes
   291→    
   292→    # Video MIME types to check in content-type header
   293→    VIDEO_MIME_TYPES = {'video/mp4', 'video/avi', 'video/quicktime', 'video/x-msvideo', 
   294→                        'video/x-matroska', 'video/webm', 'video/x-m4v', 'video/x-flv', 
   295→                        'video/x-ms-wmv', 'video/mpeg'}
   296→    
   297→    try:
   298→        # Extract filename from URL
   299→        parsed_url = urlparse(external_url)
   300→        filename = Path(parsed_url.path).name
   301→        
   302→        # Download the content and capture content-type
   303→        logger.info(f"Downloading content from: {external_url}")
   304→        response = await client.get(external_url, timeout=60)
   305→        response.raise_for_status()
   306→        file_data = response.content
   307→        content_type = response.headers.get('content-type', 'application/octet-stream').split(';')[0].strip().lower()
   308→        logger.info(f"Downloaded {len(file_data)} bytes, content-type: {content_type}")
   309→        
   310→        # If no filename in URL, create one based on task_id and content-type
   311→        if not filename or filename == '/' or '.' not in filename:
   312→            ext = mimetypes.guess_extension(content_type) or '.bin'
   313→            filename = f"task_{task_id}{ext}"
   314→            logger.info(f"Generated filename from content-type: {filename}")
   315→        
   316→        # Check if this is a video file using BOTH content-type and filename extension
   317→        is_video = content_type in VIDEO_MIME_TYPES or is_video_file(filename)
   318→        
   319→        # Extract screenshot if requested and this is a video
   320→        first_frame_data = None
   321→        if extract_screenshot and is_video:
   322→            logger.info(f"Video detected ({len(file_data)} bytes, content-type={content_type}), extracting first frame screenshot for task {task_id}")
   323→            screenshot_bytes = extract_first_frame_bytes(file_data)
   324→            if screenshot_bytes:
   325→                # Convert to base64 for inclusion in main upload (matching original approach)
   326→                first_frame_data = base64.b64encode(screenshot_bytes).decode('utf-8')
   327→                logger.info(f"First frame extracted and will be included in upload")
   328→            else:
   329→                logger.warning(f"Failed to extract first frame from video for task {task_id}")
   330→        
   331→        # Upload to Supabase storage (with first frame data if available)
   332→        supabase_url = await upload_to_supabase_storage(
   333→            client, task_id, file_data, filename, first_frame_data
   334→        )
   335→        
   336→        result = {"url": supabase_url}
   337→        if first_frame_data:
   338→            result["screenshot_included"] = True
   339→        
   340→        logger.info(f"Successfully migrated {external_url} -> {supabase_url}")
   341→        return result
   342→        
   343→    except Exception as e:
   344→        logger.error(f"Failed to download and upload {external_url}: {e}")
   345→        # Return original URL as fallback, but mark it as failed so caller knows
   346→        logger.warning(f"Falling back to original URL: {external_url}")
   347→        return {"url": external_url, "upload_failed": True, "error": str(e)}
   348→
   349→
   350→async def process_external_url_result(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   351→    """
   352→    Process a task result that contains an external URL, downloading and re-uploading to Supabase.
   353→    Automatically extracts first frame screenshots for video files.
   354→    Updates the result dict in place and returns it.
   355→    """
   356→    if not result.get("output_url"):
   357→        return result
   358→    
   359→    external_url = result["output_url"]
   360→    logger.info(f"Processing external URL for task {task_id}: {external_url}")
   361→    
   362→    try:
   363→        # Download and upload with automatic screenshot extraction
   364→        upload_result = await download_and_upload_to_supabase(client, task_id, external_url)
   365→        
   366→        # Update result with new URLs
   367→        result["output_location"] = upload_result["url"]
   368→        result["original_external_url"] = external_url
   369→        
   370→        # Add screenshot URL if extracted
   371→        if "screenshot_url" in upload_result:
   372→            result["screenshot_url"] = upload_result["screenshot_url"]
   373→        
   374→        # Check if upload actually failed (download_and_upload_to_supabase returns fallback on error)
   375→        if upload_result.get("upload_failed"):
   376→            logger.warning(f"Upload failed for task {task_id}, will need explicit mark_complete: {upload_result.get('error')}")
   377→            result["migration_error"] = upload_result.get("error")
   378→            # Do NOT set _task_completed_by_upload - let the caller handle completion
   379→        else:
   380→            # Mark that task completion was handled by the upload
   381→            result["_task_completed_by_upload"] = True
   382→            logger.info(f"Successfully processed external URL for task {task_id}")
   383→        
   384→    except Exception as e:
   385→        logger.error(f"Failed to process external URL {external_url} for task {task_id}: {e}")
   386→        # Fallback to original external URL
   387→        result["output_location"] = external_url
   388→        result["migration_error"] = str(e)
   389→        logger.warning(f"Using original external URL as fallback: {external_url}")
   390→    
   391→    return result
   392→
```

```
1→"""fal.ai-backed task handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import asyncio
     6→import logging
     7→from typing import Any, Dict
     8→
     9→import httpx
    10→
    11→from api_orchestrator.fal_utils import FalRetryConfig, build_fal_lora_list, call_fal_api_resilient
    12→from api_orchestrator.image_utils import get_image_dimensions, scale_dimensions_to_megapixels
    13→from api_orchestrator.storage_utils import process_external_url_result
    14→from api_orchestrator.task_utils import get_task_metadata, update_task_metadata
    15→

    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→async def handle_image_upscale(
    21→    task: Dict[str, Any],
    22→    params: Dict[str, Any],
    23→    client: httpx.AsyncClient
    24→) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    25→    """Handle image-upscale task type via fal.ai API with resilient request tracking."""
    26→    task_id = task.get("task_id") or task.get("id")
    27→    task_type = task.get("task_type", "image-upscale")
    28→
    29→    logger.info(f"Processing {task_type} task via fal.ai API (resilient mode)")
    30→
    31→    # Extract parameters - support both "image_url" and "image" parameter names
    32→    image_url = params.get("image_url") or params.get("image", "")
    33→    # Support both "upscale_factor" and "scale_factor" parameter names
    34→    upscale_factor = params.get("upscale_factor") or params.get("scale_factor", 2)
    35→    # Noise scale for the generation process (default 0.1)
    36→    noise_scale = params.get("noise_scale", 0.1)
    37→    # Output format - normalize "jpeg" to "jpg" for fal API
    38→    output_format = params.get("output_format", "jpg")
    39→    if output_format == "jpeg":
    40→        output_format = "jpg"
    41→    # Optional seed for reproducibility
    42→    seed = params.get("seed")
    43→
    44→    if not image_url:
    45→        logger.error(f"Missing image parameter. Available params: {list(params.keys())}")
    46→        raise Exception("image_url or image parameter is required for image-upscale task")
    47→
    48→    logger.info(f"Upscaling image: {image_url} with factor: {upscale_factor}, noise_scale: {noise_scale}")
    49→
    50→    try:
    51→        endpoint = "fal-ai/seedvr/upscale/image"
    52→        fal_args = {
    53→            "image_url": image_url,
    54→            "upscale_factor": upscale_factor,
    55→            "noise_scale": noise_scale,
    56→            "output_format": output_format,
    57→        }
    58→        if seed is not None:
    59→            fal_args["seed"] = seed
    60→        
    61→        # Check for existing tracking from previous attempt
    62→        existing_tracking = await get_fal_tracking(task_id)
    63→        if existing_tracking:
    64→            logger.info(f"Found existing request: {existing_tracking.request_id}")
    65→        
    66→        # Create update function
    67→        async def update_metadata(tid: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
    68→            return await update_task_metadata(tid, data)
    69→        
    70→        result = await call_fal_api_resilient(
    71→            endpoint=endpoint,
    72→            arguments=fal_args,
    73→            client=client,
    74→            task_id=task_id,
    75→            update_task_metadata=update_metadata,
    76→            existing_tracking=existing_tracking,
    77→        )
    78→
    79→        # Process external URL with automatic download and upload to Supabase
    80→        result = await process_external_url_result(client, task_id, result)
    81→
    82→        logger.info(f"Processed {task_type} task via fal.ai API")
    83→        return result
    84→
    85→    except Exception as e:
    86→        logger.error(f"fal.ai API call failed: {e}")
    87→        raise Exception(f"fal.ai upscale failed: {str(e)}")
    88→

    90→async def handle_qwen_image(
    91→    task: Dict[str, Any],
    92→    params: Dict[str, Any],
    93→    client: httpx.AsyncClient
    94→) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    95→    """Handle qwen_image, qwen_image_2512, and z_image_turbo task types via fal.ai API with resilient tracking."""
    96→    task_id = task.get("task_id") or task.get("id")
    97→    task_type = task.get("task_type", "qwen_image")
    98→
    99→    # Maps task_type to fal.ai endpoint base path
   100→    FALAI_IMAGE_MODELS = {
   101→        "qwen_image": "fal-ai/qwen-image",
   102→        "qwen_image_2512": "fal-ai/qwen-image-2512",
   103→        "z_image_turbo": "fal-ai/z-image/turbo",
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    endpoint = FALAI_IMAGE_MODELS[task_type]
   107→    logger.info(f"Processing {task_type} task via fal.ai API (resilient mode)")
   108→    logger.info(f"Using endpoint: {endpoint}")
   109→
   110→    # Build LoRA list from params (same format as qwen_image_style)
   111→    # Supports: loras=[{"path": ..., "scale": ...}] or additional_loras={path: scale}
   112→    loras = build_fal_lora_list(params)
   113→    if loras:
   114→        logger.info(f"Including {len(loras)} LoRAs in request")
   115→
   116→    # Build arguments - following similar naming to qwen_image_style
   117→    fal_args = {
   118→        "prompt": params.get("prompt", ""),
   119→        "seed": params.get("seed", -1),
   120→    }
   121→
   122→    # Add size/resolution if provided
   123→    # fal.ai accepts either preset strings or {"width": x, "height": y}
   124→    resolution = params.get("resolution", "")
   125→    if resolution:
   126→        # Parse resolution string like "512x512" or "1920*1080"
   127→        parts = resolution.replace("*", "x").split("x")
   128→        if len(parts) == 2:
   129→            try:
   130→                width = int(parts[0])
   131→                height = int(parts[1])
   132→                fal_args["image_size"] = {"width": width, "height": height}
   133→                logger.info(f"Using resolution: {width}x{height}")
   134→            except ValueError:
   135→                logger.warning(f"Could not parse resolution '{resolution}', using default")
   136→
   137→    # Add negative prompt if provided
   138→    negative_prompt = params.get("negative_prompt", "")
   139→    if negative_prompt:
   140→        fal_args["negative_prompt"] = negative_prompt
   141→
   142→    # Add LoRAs to args if provided
   143→    if loras:
   144→        fal_args["loras"] = loras
   145→
   146→    try:
   147→        # Check for existing tracking from previous attempt
   148→        existing_tracking = await get_fal_tracking(task_id)
   149→        if existing_tracking:
   150→            logger.info(f"Found existing request: {existing_tracking.request_id}")
   151→        
   152→        # Create update function
   153→        async def update_metadata(tid: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
   154→            return await update_task_metadata(tid, data)
   155→        
   156→        result = await call_fal_api_resilient(
   157→            endpoint=endpoint,
   158→            arguments=fal_args,
   159→            client=client,
   160→            task_id=task_id,
   161→            update_task_metadata=update_metadata,
   162→            existing_tracking=existing_tracking,
   163→        )
   164→        result = await process_external_url_result(client, task_id, result)
   165→        logger.info(f"Processed {task_type} task via fal.ai API")
   166→        return result
   167→    except Exception as e:
   168→        logger.error(f"fal.ai API call failed: {e}")
   169→        raise Exception(f"fal.ai {task_type} failed: {str(e)}")
   170→
   171→
   172→async def handle_z_image_turbo_i2i(
   173→    task: Dict[str, Any],
   174→    params: Dict[str, Any],
   175→    client: httpx.AsyncClient
   176→) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   177→    """Handle z_image_turbo_i2i task type via fal.ai API with resilient tracking."""
   178→    task_id = task.get("task_id") or task.get("id")
   179→    task_type = task.get("task_type", "z_image_turbo_i2i")
   180→
   181→    logger.info(f"Processing {task_type} task via fal.ai API (resilient mode)")
   182→
   183→    # Build LoRA list from params
   184→    loras = build_fal_lora_list(params)
   185→
   186→    # Select endpoint based on whether LoRAs are provided
   187→    if loras:
   188→        endpoint = "fal-ai/z-image/turbo/image-to-image/lora"
   189→        logger.info(f"Using LoRA endpoint with {len(loras)} LoRAs")
   190→    else:
   191→        endpoint = "fal-ai/z-image/turbo/image-to-image"
   192→        logger.info("Using standard endpoint (no LoRAs)")
   193→
   194→    # Get required image_url
   195→    image_url = params.get("image_url") or params.get("image")
   196→    if not image_url:
   197→        raise Exception("image_url or image parameter is required for z_image_turbo_i2i task")
   198→
   199→    # Determine output image_size
   200→    # If explicit image_size is provided (not "auto"), use it directly
   201→    # Otherwise, fetch input dimensions and scale to target megapixels
   202→    explicit_image_size = params.get("image_size")
   203→    if explicit_image_size and explicit_image_size != "auto":
   204→        # User provided explicit size - use as-is
   205→        image_size = explicit_image_size
   206→        logger.info(f"Using explicit image_size: {image_size}")
   207→    else:
   208→        # Auto-scale to target megapixels (default ~1MP = 1024x1024 equivalent)
   209→        target_mp = params.get("target_megapixels", 1.0)
   210→        dimensions = await get_image_dimensions(client, image_url)
   211→
   212→        if dimensions:
   213→            width, height = dimensions
   214→            image_size = scale_dimensions_to_megapixels(width, height, target_megapixels=target_mp)
   215→            logger.info(f"Auto-scaled to {image_size['width']}x{image_size['height']} (~{target_mp}MP)")
   216→        else:
   217→            # Fallback to "auto" if we couldn't get dimensions
   218→            image_size = "auto"
   219→            logger.warning("Could not get input dimensions, falling back to image_size='auto'")
   220→
   221→    # Build arguments
   222→    fal_args = {
   223→        "prompt": params.get("prompt", ""),
   224→        "image_url": image_url,
   225→        "strength": params.get("strength", 0.6),
   226→        "image_size": image_size,
   227→        "num_inference_steps": params.get("num_inference_steps", 8),
   228→        "num_images": params.get("num_images", 1),
   229→        "enable_safety_checker": params.get("enable_safety_checker", True),
   230→        "output_format": params.get("output_format", "png"),
   231→        "acceleration": params.get("acceleration", "none" if loras else "high"),
   232→    }
   233→
   234→    # Add LoRAs for LoRA endpoint calls.
   235→    if loras:
   236→        fal_args["loras"] = loras
   237→
   238→    # Add optional seed if provided
   239→    seed = params.get("seed")
   240→    if seed is not None:
   241→        fal_args["seed"] = seed
   242→
   243→    # Add negative prompt if provided
   244→    negative_prompt = params.get("negative_prompt", "")
   245→    if negative_prompt:
   246→        fal_args["negative_prompt"] = negative_prompt
   247→
   248→    logger.info(f"Z-Image Turbo i2i: image={image_url}, strength={fal_args['strength']}, steps={fal_args['num_inference_steps']}, size={image_size}")
   249→
   250→    try:
   251→        # Check for existing tracking from previous attempt
   252→        existing_tracking = await get_fal_tracking(task_id)
   253→        if existing_tracking:
   254→            logger.info(f"Found existing request: {existing_tracking.request_id}")
   255→        
   256→        # Create update function
   257→        async def update_metadata(tid: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
   258→            return await update_task_metadata(tid, data)
   259→        
   260→        result = await call_fal_api_resilient(
   261→            endpoint=endpoint,
   262→            arguments=fal_args,
   263→            client=client,
   264→            task_id=task_id,
   265→            update_task_metadata=update_metadata,
   266→            existing_tracking=existing_tracking,
   267→        )
   268→        result = await process_external_url_result(client, task_id, result)
   269→        logger.info(f"Processed {task_type} task via fal.ai API")
   270→        return result
   271→    except Exception as e:
   272→        logger.error(f"fal.ai API call failed: {e}")
   273→        raise Exception(f"fal.ai {task_type} failed: {str(e)}")
   274→
   275→
   276→async def handle_video_enhance(
   277→    task: Dict[str, Any],
   278→    params: Dict[str, Any],
   279→    client: httpx.AsyncClient
   280→) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   281→    """Handle video_enhance task type via fal.ai API with resilient request tracking.
   282→    
   283→    Supports two enhancement operations that can run independently or chained:
   284→    - Interpolation: Frame interpolation using Google FILM (fal-ai/film/video)
   285→    - Upscale: Video upscaling using FlashVSR (fal-ai/flashvsr/upscale/video)
   286→    
   287→    When both are enabled, interpolation runs first, then upscale on the result.
   288→    
   289→    This handler uses resilient fal.ai calls that:
   290→    - Track request IDs in task metadata for recovery
   291→    - Check for existing completed jobs before resubmitting
   292→    - Use extended retries for 503 "Runner disconnected" errors
   293→    
   294→    Parameters:
   295→        Common:
   296→            - video_url: URL of the video to enhance (required)
   297→            - enable_interpolation: Enable frame interpolation (default: false)
   298→            - enable_upscale: Enable video upscaling (default: false)
   299→        
   300→        Interpolation (nested in "interpolation" object):
   301→            - num_frames: 1-4, frames added between each pair (default: 1)
   302→            - use_calculated_fps: Maintain same playback speed (default: true)
   303→            - video_quality: "low"/"medium"/"high"/"maximum" (default: "high")
   304→            - fps: Output FPS if use_calculated_fps is false (default: 8)
   305→            - use_scene_detection: Split video into scenes before interpolation (optional)
   306→            - loop: Loop final frame back to first (optional)
   307→            - video_write_mode: "fast"/"balanced"/"small" (default: "balanced")
   308→        
   309→        Upscale (nested in "upscale" object):
   310→            - upscale_factor: 1.5-4x scaling factor (default: 2)
   311→            - color_fix: Apply color correction (default: true)
   312→            - output_quality: "low"/"medium"/"high"/"maximum" (default: "high")
   313→            - acceleration: "regular"/"high"/"full" - faster = longer videos supported (default: "regular")
   314→            - quality: 0-100 tile blending quality (default: 70)
   315→            - preserve_audio: Copy original audio to output (optional)
   316→            - output_format: "X264 (.mp4)"/"VP9 (.webm)"/"PRORES4444 (.mov)"/"GIF (.gif)"
   317→            - output_write_mode: "fast"/"balanced"/"small" (default: "balanced")
   318→            - seed: Random seed for reproducibility (optional)
   319→    """
   320→    task_id = task.get("task_id") or task.get("id")
   321→    task_type = task.get("task_type", "video_enhance")
   322→
   323→    logger.info(f"Processing {task_type} task via fal.ai API (resilient mode)")
   324→
   325→    # Get required video_url
   326→    video_url = params.get("video_url") or params.get("video")
   327→    if not video_url:
   328→        raise Exception("video_url or video parameter is required for video_enhance task")
   329→
   330→    # Check which operations are enabled
   331→    enable_interpolation = params.get("enable_interpolation", False)
   332→    enable_upscale = params.get("enable_upscale", False)
   333→
   334→    if not enable_interpolation and not enable_upscale:
   335→        raise Exception("At least one of enable_interpolation or enable_upscale must be true")
   336→
   337→    # Get nested parameter objects
   338→    interpolation_params = params.get("interpolation", {})
   339→    upscale_params = params.get("upscale", {})
   340→
   341→    current_video_url = video_url
   342→    result = None
   343→    operations_performed = []
   344→    
   345→    # Configuration for video processing - longer timeouts since videos take time
   346→    video_config = FalRetryConfig(
   347→        poll_timeout_sec=900.0,  # 15 minutes for video processing
   348→        poll_progress_log_interval_sec=30.0,  # Log every 30s
   349→        fetch_max_attempts=25,  # More attempts for result fetch
   350→        fetch_base_delay_sec=5.0,
   351→        fetch_max_delay_sec=60.0,
   352→    )
   353→
   354→    try:
   355→        # Step 1: Interpolation (if enabled) - runs first to add frames
   356→        if enable_interpolation:
   357→            endpoint = "fal-ai/film/video"
   358→            logger.info(f"Step 1: Running FILM interpolation on {current_video_url}")
   359→
   360→            fal_args = {
   361→                "video_url": current_video_url,
   362→                "num_frames": interpolation_params.get("num_frames", 1),
   363→                "use_calculated_fps": interpolation_params.get("use_calculated_fps", True),
   364→                "video_quality": interpolation_params.get("video_quality", "high"),
   365→            }
   366→
   367→            # Optional FILM parameters
   368→            if "fps" in interpolation_params:
   369→                fal_args["fps"] = interpolation_params["fps"]
   370→            if "use_scene_detection" in interpolation_params:
   371→                fal_args["use_scene_detection"] = interpolation_params["use_scene_detection"]
   372→            if "loop" in interpolation_params:
   373→                fal_args["loop"] = interpolation_params["loop"]
   374→            if "video_write_mode" in interpolation_params:
   375→                fal_args["video_write_mode"] = interpolation_params["video_write_mode"]
   376→
   377→            logger.info(f"FILM interpolation: num_frames={fal_args['num_frames']}, "
   378→                       f"use_calculated_fps={fal_args['use_calculated_fps']}, "
   379→                       f"video_quality={fal_args['video_quality']}")
   380→
   381→            # Check for existing tracking from previous attempt
   382→            existing_tracking = await get_fal_tracking(task_id, step_key="interpolation")
   383→            if existing_tracking:
   384→                logger.info(f"Found existing interpolation request: {existing_tracking.request_id}")
   385→            
   386→            # Create step-specific update function
   387→            async def update_interpolation_metadata(tid: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
   388→                return await update_fal_tracking(tid, data, step_key="interpolation")
   389→            
   390→            result = await call_fal_api_resilient(
   391→                endpoint=endpoint,
   392→                arguments=fal_args,
   393→                client=client,
   394→                task_id=task_id,
   395→                update_task_metadata=update_interpolation_metadata,
   396→                existing_tracking=existing_tracking,
   397→                config=video_config,
   398→            )
   399→            operations_performed.append("interpolation")
   400→
   401→            # If upscale is also enabled, use the interpolated video as input
   402→            if enable_upscale and result.get("output_url"):
   403→                current_video_url = result["output_url"]
   404→                logger.info(f"Interpolation complete, chaining to upscale with: {current_video_url}")
   405→                
   406→                # Store the intermediate URL in metadata for recovery
   407→                await update_task_metadata(task_id, {
   408→                    'interpolation_output_url': current_video_url,
   409→                    'interpolation_completed_at': asyncio.get_event_loop().time(),
   410→                })
   411→
   412→        # Step 2: Upscale (if enabled) - runs on original or interpolated video
   413→        if enable_upscale:
   414→            endpoint = "fal-ai/flashvsr/upscale/video"
   415→            
   416→            # Check if we have an interpolation result URL from a previous run
   417→            if enable_interpolation and not result:
   418→                # Try to recover interpolation output from metadata
   419→                metadata = await get_task_metadata(task_id)
   420→                if metadata and metadata.get('interpolation_output_url'):
   421→                    current_video_url = metadata['interpolation_output_url']
   422→                    logger.info(f"Recovered interpolation output URL from metadata: {current_video_url}")
   423→                    operations_performed.append("interpolation")  # Mark as done
   424→            
   425→            logger.info(f"Step 2: Running FlashVSR upscale on {current_video_url}")
   426→
   427→            fal_args = {
   428→                "video_url": current_video_url,
   429→                "upscale_factor": upscale_params.get("upscale_factor", 2),
   430→                "color_fix": upscale_params.get("color_fix", True),
   431→                "output_quality": upscale_params.get("output_quality", "high"),
   432→            }
   433→
   434→            # Optional FlashVSR parameters
   435→            if "acceleration" in upscale_params:
   436→                fal_args["acceleration"] = upscale_params["acceleration"]  # regular/high/full
   437→            if "quality" in upscale_params:
   438→                fal_args["quality"] = upscale_params["quality"]  # 0-100 tile blending
   439→            if "preserve_audio" in upscale_params:
   440→                fal_args["preserve_audio"] = upscale_params["preserve_audio"]
   441→            if "output_format" in upscale_params:
   442→                fal_args["output_format"] = upscale_params["output_format"]
   443→            if "output_write_mode" in upscale_params:
   444→                fal_args["output_write_mode"] = upscale_params["output_write_mode"]
   445→            if "seed" in upscale_params:
   446→                fal_args["seed"] = upscale_params["seed"]
   447→
   448→            logger.info(f"FlashVSR upscale: factor={fal_args['upscale_factor']}, "
   449→                       f"color_fix={fal_args['color_fix']}, output_quality={fal_args['output_quality']}")
   450→
   451→            # Check for existing tracking from previous attempt
   452→            existing_tracking = await get_fal_tracking(task_id, step_key="upscale")
   453→            if existing_tracking:
   454→                logger.info(f"Found existing upscale request: {existing_tracking.request_id}")
   455→            
   456→            # Create step-specific update function
   457→            async def update_upscale_metadata(tid: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
   458→                return await update_fal_tracking(tid, data, step_key="upscale")
   459→            
   460→            result = await call_fal_api_resilient(
   461→                endpoint=endpoint,
   462→                arguments=fal_args,
   463→                client=client,
   464→                task_id=task_id,
   465→                update_task_metadata=update_upscale_metadata,
   466→                existing_tracking=existing_tracking,
   467→                config=video_config,
   468→            )
   469→            operations_performed.append("upscale")
   470→
   471→        # Process final result - download and upload to Supabase
   472→        result = await process_external_url_result(client, task_id, result)
   473→        result["operations_performed"] = operations_performed
   474→        
   475→        logger.info(f"Processed {task_type} task via fal.ai API: {', '.join(operations_performed)}")
   476→        return result
   477→
   478→    except Exception as e:
   479→        logger.error(f"fal.ai API call failed during {operations_performed or ['setup']}: {e}")
   480→        raise Exception(f"fal.ai {task_type} failed during {operations_performed or ['setup']}: {str(e)}")
   481→
   482→
   483→__all__ = [
   484→    "handle_image_upscale",
   485→    "handle_qwen_image",
   486→    "handle_z_image_turbo_i2i",
   487→    "handle_video_enhance",
   488→]
   489→
```

```
1→"""
     2→Video processing utilities for the API orchestrator.
     3→Handles video file detection, first frame extraction, and screenshot generation.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import os
     7→import logging
     8→import tempfile
     9→import subprocess
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import Optional, List
    12→
    13→import cv2
    14→import httpx
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→def is_video_file(filename: str) -> bool:
    20→    """Check if a filename indicates a video file based on extension."""
    21→    video_extensions = {'.mp4', '.avi', '.mov', '.mkv', '.webm', '.m4v', '.flv', '.wmv'}
    22→    return Path(filename).suffix.lower() in video_extensions
    23→
    24→
    25→def save_frame_from_video(video_path: Path, frame_index: int, output_image_path: Path, resolution: tuple[int, int]) -> bool:
    26→    """
    27→    Extracts a specific frame from video and saves as image.
    28→    Matches the original travel_between_images.py implementation.
    29→    """
    30→    if not video_path.exists() or video_path.stat().st_size == 0:
    31→        logger.error(f"Video file not found or empty: {video_path}")
    32→        return False
    33→
    34→    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_path))
    35→    if not cap.isOpened():
    36→        logger.error(f"Could not open video file: {video_path}")
    37→        return False
    38→
    39→    total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
    40→
    41→    # Support negative indexing (e.g., -1 for last frame)
    42→    if frame_index < 0:
    43→        frame_index = total_frames + frame_index
    44→
    45→    if frame_index < 0 or frame_index >= total_frames:
    46→        logger.error(f"Frame index {frame_index} out of bounds (total: {total_frames})")
    47→        cap.release()
    48→        return False
    49→
    50→    # Seek to specific frame and extract
    51→    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, float(frame_index))
    52→    ret, frame = cap.read()
    53→    cap.release()
    54→
    55→    if not ret or frame is None:
    56→        logger.error(f"Could not read frame {frame_index}")
    57→        return False
    58→
    59→    try:
    60→        # Resize frame if needed (matching original implementation)
    61→        if frame.shape[1] != resolution[0] or frame.shape[0] != resolution[1]:
    62→            frame = cv2.resize(frame, resolution, interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    63→        
    64→        # Save frame as image
    65→        output_image_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    66→        cv2.imwrite(str(output_image_path), frame)
    67→        logger.info(f"Successfully saved frame {frame_index} to {output_image_path}")
    68→        return True
    69→        
    70→    except Exception as e:
    71→        logger.error(f"Error saving frame: {e}")
    72→        return False
    73→
    74→
    75→def extract_first_frame_bytes(video_data: bytes) -> Optional[bytes]:
    76→    """
    77→    Extract the first frame from video data and return it as PNG bytes.
    78→    Uses the save_frame_from_video function to match original implementation.
    79→    """
    80→    try:
    81→        # Create temporary file for video processing
    82→        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp4', delete=False) as temp_video:
    83→            temp_video.write(video_data)
    84→            temp_video_path = temp_video.name
    85→
    86→        try:
    87→            # Get video dimensions first (matching original approach)
    88→            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(temp_video_path)
    89→            if not cap.isOpened():
    90→                logger.error("Could not open video file for first frame extraction")
    91→                return None
    92→                
    93→            width = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
    94→            height = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
    95→            cap.release()
    96→            
    97→            if width <= 0 or height <= 0:
    98→                logger.error("Invalid video dimensions")
    99→                return None
   100→
   101→            # Create temporary file for screenshot
   102→            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.png', delete=False) as temp_screenshot:
   103→                temp_screenshot_path = temp_screenshot.name
   104→
   105→            try:
   106→                # Use the original-style function to extract frame
   107→                if save_frame_from_video(Path(temp_video_path), 0, Path(temp_screenshot_path), (width, height)):
   108→                    # Read screenshot data
   109→                    with open(temp_screenshot_path, 'rb') as f:
   110→                        screenshot_data = f.read()
   111→                    return screenshot_data
   112→                else:
   113→                    return None
   114→
   115→            finally:
   116→                # Clean up temporary screenshot file
   117→                try:
   118→                    os.unlink(temp_screenshot_path)
   119→                except Exception:
   120→                    pass
   121→
   122→        finally:
   123→            # Clean up temporary video file
   124→            try:
   125→                os.unlink(temp_video_path)
   126→            except Exception:
   127→                pass
   128→
   129→    except Exception as e:
   130→        logger.error(f"Failed to extract first frame from video: {e}")

   133→
   134→def remove_last_frame_from_video(video_path: str, output_path: str) -> bool:
   135→    """
   136→    Remove the last frame from a video using ffmpeg.
   137→    Returns True if successful, False otherwise.
   138→    """
   139→    try:
   140→        # Get video info to determine frame count
   141→        probe_cmd = [
   142→            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error', '-select_streams', 'v:0',
   143→            '-count_frames', '-show_entries', 'stream=nb_frames',
   144→            '-of', 'csv=p=0', video_path
   145→        ]
   146→        
   147→        result = subprocess.run(probe_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, timeout=60)
   148→        total_frames = int(result.stdout.strip())
   149→        
   150→        if total_frames <= 1:
   151→            logger.warning(f"Video has {total_frames} frames, cannot remove last frame")
   152→            return False
   153→        
   154→        # Remove last frame using ffmpeg
   155→        frames_to_keep = total_frames - 1
   156→        cmd = [
   157→            'ffmpeg', '-i', video_path, '-vf', f'select=lt(n\\,{frames_to_keep})',
   158→            '-vsync', 'vfr', '-y', output_path
   159→        ]
   160→        
   161→        subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True, timeout=300)
   162→        logger.info(f"Successfully removed last frame from {video_path}, saved to {output_path}")
   163→        return True
   164→        
   165→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   166→        logger.error(f"FFmpeg timed out removing last frame from {video_path}")
   167→        return False
   168→    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   169→        logger.error(f"FFmpeg error removing last frame: {e.stderr}")
   170→        return False
   171→    except Exception as e:
   172→        logger.error(f"Error removing last frame from video: {e}")
   173→        return False
   174→
   175→
   176→def join_videos(video_paths: List[str], output_path: str) -> bool:
   177→    """
   178→    Join multiple videos together using ffmpeg concat filter.
   179→    Returns True if successful, False otherwise.
   180→    """
   181→    if not video_paths:
   182→        logger.error("No video paths provided for joining")
   183→        return False
   184→    
   185→    if len(video_paths) == 1:
   186→        # Just copy the single video
   187→        try:
   188→            subprocess.run(['cp', video_paths[0], output_path], check=True)
   189→            logger.info(f"Single video copied to {output_path}")
   190→            return True
   191→        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   192→            logger.error(f"Error copying single video: {e}")
   193→            return False
   194→    
   195→    try:
   196→        # Create filter complex for concatenation
   197→        filter_parts = []
   198→        for i in range(len(video_paths)):
   199→            filter_parts.append(f"[{i}:v]")
   200→        
   201→        filter_complex = f"{''.join(filter_parts)}concat=n={len(video_paths)}:v=1:a=0[outv]"
   202→        
   203→        # Build ffmpeg command
   204→        cmd = ['ffmpeg']
   205→        for video_path in video_paths:
   206→            cmd.extend(['-i', video_path])
   207→        
   208→        cmd.extend([
   209→            '-filter_complex', filter_complex,
   210→            '-map', '[outv]',
   211→            '-y', output_path
   212→        ])
   213→        
   214→        subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True, timeout=300)
   215→        logger.info(f"Successfully joined {len(video_paths)} videos to {output_path}")
   216→        return True
   217→        
   218→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   219→        logger.error(f"FFmpeg timed out joining {len(video_paths)} videos")
   220→        return False
   221→    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
   222→        logger.error(f"FFmpeg error joining videos: {e.stderr}")
   223→        return False
   224→    except Exception as e:
   225→        logger.error(f"Error joining videos: {e}")
   226→        return False
   227→
   228→
   229→async def download_video_to_temp(client: httpx.AsyncClient, video_url: str) -> Optional[str]:
   230→    """
   231→    Download a video from URL to a temporary file.
   232→    Returns the temporary file path if successful, None otherwise.
   233→    """
   234→    try:
   235→        response = await client.get(video_url)
   236→        response.raise_for_status()
   237→        
   238→        # Create temporary file
   239→        temp_file = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp4', delete=False)
   240→        temp_file.write(response.content)
   241→        temp_file.close()
   242→        
   243→        logger.info(f"Downloaded video from {video_url} to {temp_file.name}")
   244→        return temp_file.name
   245→        
   246→    except Exception as e:
   247→        logger.error(f"Error downloading video from {video_url}: {e}")
   248→        return None
   249→
```

```
180→    async def spawn_task(task_payload: Dict[str, Any], client: httpx.AsyncClient):
   181→        task_id = task_payload.get("task_id") or task_payload.get("id")
   182→
   183→        # Set task context for logging
   184→        set_current_task(str(task_id))
   185→
   186→        try:
   187→            logger.info(f"Starting task {task_id}")
   188→            result = await process_api_task(task_payload, client)
   189→
   190→            # Check if task completion was already handled by the upload process
   191→            if result.get("_task_completed_by_upload"):
   192→                logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completion already handled by upload process, skipping additional mark_complete call")
   193→            else:
   194→                # Only call mark_complete if the upload process didn't handle it
   195→                success = await mark_complete(client, task_id, result)
   196→                if not success:
   197→                    # If we can't mark the task complete, treat it as a failure to prevent stuck tasks
   198→                    logger.error(f"Task {task_id} processed successfully but failed to save to database")
   199→                    await mark_failed(client, task_id, "Task processed successfully but failed to save completion status to database")
   200→                else:
   201→                    logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completed successfully")
   202→        except Exception as exc:
   203→            logger.error(f"Task {task_id} failed with exception: {exc}")
   204→            logger.error(f"Exception type: {type(exc).__name__}")
   205→            import traceback
   206→            logger.error(f"Full traceback: {traceback.format_exc()}")
   207→            failed_success = await mark_failed(client, task_id, str(exc))
   208→            if not failed_success:
   209→                logger.error(f"DOUBLE FAILURE: Task {task_id} failed AND could not mark as failed in database!")
   210→        finally:
   211→            # Clear task context
   212→            set_current_task(None)
   213→
   214→    async with httpx.AsyncClient(limits=limits, timeout=20.0) as client:
```
