```
1→"""Periodic maintenance checks (storage, stale workers, zombie reconciliation)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import os
     7→from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
     8→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Dict, List
     9→
    10→import runpod
    11→from gpu_orchestrator.control.diagnostics import WorkerDiagnosticsMixin
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from gpu_orchestrator.control.contracts import ControlHost
    17→
    18→
    19→class PeriodicChecksMixin:
    20→    """Periodic check methods executed every cycle or every N cycles."""
    21→
    22→    async def _run_periodic_checks(
    23→        self: "ControlHost",
    24→        worker_states: List[Any],
    25→        summary: Dict[str, Any],
    26→    ) -> None:
    27→        """Run checks that only happen every Nth cycle."""
    28→        if not isinstance(self, WorkerDiagnosticsMixin):
    29→            logger.debug("Periodic checks host is missing diagnostics mixin methods")
    30→
    31→        periodic_summary: Dict[str, Any] = {
    32→            "actions": {
    33→                "tasks_reset": 0,
    34→                "workers_failed": 0,
    35→                "workers_terminated": 0,
    36→            },
    37→            "storage_health": {},
    38→        }
    39→
    40→        active_workers = []
    41→        for ws in worker_states:
    42→            if ws.is_active:
    43→                worker = await self.db.get_worker_by_id(ws.worker_id)
    44→                if worker:
    45→                    active_workers.append(worker)
    46→
    47→        await self._check_storage_health(active_workers, periodic_summary)
    48→
    49→        all_workers = []
    50→        for ws in worker_states:
    51→            worker = await self.db.get_worker_by_id(ws.worker_id)
    52→            if worker:
    53→                all_workers.append(worker)
    54→
    55→        await self._failsafe_stale_worker_check(all_workers, periodic_summary)
    56→
    57→        summary.tasks_reset += periodic_summary["actions"].get("tasks_reset", 0)
    58→        summary.workers_failed += periodic_summary["actions"].get("workers_failed", 0)
    59→        summary.workers_terminated += periodic_summary["actions"].get("workers_terminated", 0)
    60→
    61→    async def _check_storage_health(
    62→        self: "ControlHost",
    63→        active_workers: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    64→        summary: Dict[str, Any],
    65→    ) -> None:
    66→        """Check storage health across all storage volumes."""
    67→        if self.cycle_count - self.last_storage_check_cycle < self.config.storage_check_interval_cycles:
    68→            return
    69→
    70→        self.last_storage_check_cycle = self.cycle_count
    71→        logger.info(f"STORAGE_CHECK Starting periodic check (cycle {self.cycle_count})")
    72→
    73→        workers_by_storage: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    74→        for worker in active_workers:
    75→            storage_volume = worker.get("metadata", {}).get("storage_volume")
    76→            if storage_volume:
    77→                if storage_volume not in workers_by_storage:
    78→                    workers_by_storage[storage_volume] = []
    79→                workers_by_storage[storage_volume].append(worker)
    80→
    81→        if not workers_by_storage:
    82→            logger.info("STORAGE_CHECK No active workers with storage volume info")
    83→            return
    84→
    85→        if "storage_health" not in summary:
    86→            summary["storage_health"] = {}
    87→
    88→        for storage_name, storage_workers in workers_by_storage.items():
    89→            try:
    90→                volume_id = self.runpod._get_storage_volume_id(storage_name)
    91→                if not volume_id:
    92→                    continue
    93→
    94→                check_worker = None
    95→                for worker in storage_workers:
    96→                    if worker.get("last_heartbeat"):
    97→                        try:
    98→                            heartbeat_time = datetime.fromisoformat(worker["last_heartbeat"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    99→                            heartbeat_age = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - heartbeat_time).total_seconds()
   100→                            if heartbeat_age < 60:
   101→                                runpod_id = worker.get("metadata", {}).get("runpod_id")
   102→                                if runpod_id:
   103→                                    check_worker = worker
   104→                                    break
   105→                        except Exception as exc:
   106→                            logger.debug(
   107→                                "STORAGE_CHECK skipping worker heartbeat parse for %s: %s",
   108→                                worker.get("id"),
   109→                                exc,
   110→                            )
   111→                            continue
   112→
   113→                if not check_worker:
   114→                    continue
   115→
   116→                runpod_id = check_worker.get("metadata", {}).get("runpod_id")
   117→                health = self.runpod.check_storage_health(
   118→                    storage_name=storage_name,
   119→                    volume_id=volume_id,
   120→                    active_runpod_id=runpod_id,
   121→                    min_free_gb=self.config.storage_min_free_gb,
   122→                    max_percent_used=self.config.storage_max_percent_used,
   123→                )
   124→
   125→                summary["storage_health"][storage_name] = health
   126→
   127→                if health.get("needs_expansion"):
   128→                    current_size = health.get("total_gb", 100)
   129→                    new_size = current_size + self.config.storage_expansion_increment_gb
   130→
   131→                    logger.warning(f"STORAGE_CHECK '{storage_name}' needs expansion: {health.get('message')}")
   132→                    if self.runpod._expand_network_volume(volume_id, new_size):
   133→                        logger.info(f"STORAGE_CHECK Successfully expanded '{storage_name}' to {new_size}GB")
   134→                    else:
   135→                        logger.error(f"STORAGE_CHECK Failed to expand '{storage_name}'!")
   136→
   137→            except Exception as exc:
   138→                logger.error(f"STORAGE_CHECK Error checking '{storage_name}': {exc}")
   139→
   140→    async def _check_error_worker_cleanup(self: "ControlHost", worker: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   141→        """Check workers marked as error and ensure RunPod instances are terminated."""
   142→        worker_id = worker["id"]
   143→        metadata = worker.get("metadata", {})
   144→        runpod_id = metadata.get("runpod_id")
   145→        error_time_str = metadata.get("error_time")
   146→
   147→        if error_time_str:
   148→            try:
   149→                error_time = datetime.fromisoformat(error_time_str.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   150→                error_age = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - error_time).total_seconds()
   151→
   152→                if error_age > self.config.error_cleanup_grace_period_sec:
   153→                    logger.info(
   154→                        f"[ERROR_CLEANUP] Worker {worker_id} past grace period ({error_age:.0f}s), forcing cleanup"
   155→                    )
   156→
   157→                    if runpod_id:
   158→                        pod_status = self.runpod.get_pod_status(runpod_id)
   159→                        if pod_status and pod_status.get("desired_status") not in ["TERMINATED", "FAILED"]:
   160→                            try:
   161→                                self.runpod.terminate_worker(runpod_id)
   162→                            except Exception as exc:
   163→                                logger.error(f"Failed to force terminate RunPod {runpod_id}: {exc}")
   164→
   165→                    termination_metadata = {"terminated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
   166→                    await self.db.update_worker_status(worker_id, "terminated", termination_metadata)
   167→
   168→            except Exception as exc:
   169→                logger.error(f"Error parsing error_time for worker {worker_id}: {exc}")
   170→        else:
   171→            logger.warning(f"[ERROR_CLEANUP] Worker {worker_id} has no error_time, forcing immediate cleanup")
   172→            if runpod_id:
   173→                try:
   174→                    self.runpod.terminate_worker(runpod_id)
   175→                    termination_metadata = {"terminated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
   176→                    await self.db.update_worker_status(worker_id, "terminated", termination_metadata)
   177→                except Exception as exc:
   178→                    logger.error(f"[ERROR_CLEANUP] Failed to cleanup legacy error worker {worker_id}: {exc}")
   179→
   180→    async def _failsafe_stale_worker_check(
   181→        self: "ControlHost",
   182→        workers: List[Dict[str, Any]],
   183→        summary: Dict[str, Any],
   184→    ) -> None:
   185→        """Failsafe check for workers with very stale heartbeats."""
   186→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   187→        active_cutoff = now - timedelta(seconds=self.config.gpu_idle_timeout_sec)
   188→        spawning_cutoff = now - timedelta(seconds=self.config.spawning_timeout_sec)
   189→
   190→        stale_workers = []
   191→
   192→        for worker in workers:
   193→            if worker["status"] == "terminated":
   194→                continue
   195→
   196→            cutoff = spawning_cutoff if worker["status"] == "spawning" else active_cutoff
   197→
   198→            last_heartbeat = worker.get("last_heartbeat")
   199→            if last_heartbeat:
   200→                try:
   201→                    heartbeat_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_heartbeat.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   202→                    if heartbeat_dt < cutoff:
   203→                        stale_workers.append(worker)
   204→                except Exception as exc:
   205→                    logger.debug(
   206→                        "FAILSAFE heartbeat parse failed for worker %s: %s",
   207→                        worker.get("id"),
   208→                        exc,
   209→                    )
   210→            else:
   211→                try:
   212→                    created_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(worker["created_at"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   213→                    if created_dt < cutoff:
   214→                        stale_workers.append(worker)
   215→                except Exception as exc:
   216→                    logger.debug(
   217→                        "FAILSAFE created_at parse failed for worker %s: %s",
   218→                        worker.get("id"),
   219→                        exc,
   220→                    )
   221→
   222→        for worker in stale_workers:
   223→            worker_id = worker["id"]
   224→            logger.warning(f"FAILSAFE: Worker {worker_id} has stale heartbeat")
   225→
   226→            try:
   227→                runpod_id = worker.get("metadata", {}).get("runpod_id")
   228→                if runpod_id:
   229→                    pod_status = self.runpod.get_pod_status(runpod_id)
   230→                    if pod_status and pod_status.get("desired_status") not in ["TERMINATED", "FAILED"]:
   231→                        self.runpod.terminate_worker(runpod_id)
   232→
   233→                reset_count = await self.db.reset_orphaned_tasks([worker_id])
   234→                if reset_count > 0:
   235→                    summary["actions"]["tasks_reset"] = summary["actions"].get("tasks_reset", 0) + reset_count
   236→
   237→                metadata_update = {
   238→                    "error_reason": f"FAILSAFE: Stale heartbeat with status {worker['status']}",
   239→                    "error_time": now.isoformat(),
   240→                }
   241→                await self.db.update_worker_status(worker_id, "terminated", metadata_update)
   242→                summary["actions"]["workers_failed"] = summary["actions"].get("workers_failed", 0) + 1
   243→
   244→            except Exception as exc:
   245→                logger.error(f"FAILSAFE: Failed to cleanup stale worker {worker_id}: {exc}")
   246→
   247→        await self._efficient_zombie_check(summary)
   248→
   249→    async def _efficient_zombie_check(self: "ControlHost", summary: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   250→        """Bidirectional zombie detection."""
   251→        if self.cycle_count % 10 != 0:
   252→            return
   253→
   254→        try:
   255→            logger.info("Running efficient zombie check (every 10th cycle)")
   256→
   257→            runpod.api_key = os.getenv("RUNPOD_API_KEY")
   258→
   259→            pods = runpod.get_pods()
   260→            gpu_worker_pods = [
   261→                pod
   262→                for pod in pods
   263→                if pod.get("name", "").startswith("gpu-")
   264→                and pod.get("desiredStatus") not in ["TERMINATED", "FAILED"]
   265→            ]
   266→
   267→            if not gpu_worker_pods:
   268→                return
   269→
   270→            db_workers = await self.db.get_workers()
   271→            db_worker_names = {w["id"] for w in db_workers if w["status"] != "terminated"}
   272→
   273→            for pod in gpu_worker_pods:
   274→                pod_name = pod.get("name")
   275→                runpod_id = pod.get("id")
   276→
   277→                if pod_name not in db_worker_names:
   278→                    logger.warning(f"ZOMBIE DETECTED: RunPod pod {pod_name} ({runpod_id}) not in database")
   279→                    try:
   280→                        success = self.runpod.terminate_worker(runpod_id)
   281→                        if success:
   282→                            summary["actions"]["workers_terminated"] = (
   283→                                summary["actions"].get("workers_terminated", 0) + 1
   284→                            )
   285→                    except Exception as exc:
   286→                        logger.error(f"ZOMBIE CLEANUP: Error terminating {runpod_id}: {exc}")
   287→
   288→            runpod_pod_ids = {pod.get("id") for pod in gpu_worker_pods}
   289→            runpod_pod_names = {pod.get("name") for pod in gpu_worker_pods}
   290→
   291→            for worker in db_workers:
   292→                if worker["status"] in ["active", "spawning"]:
   293→                    runpod_id = worker.get("metadata", {}).get("runpod_id")
   294→                    worker_id = worker["id"]
   295→
   296→                    if runpod_id and runpod_id not in runpod_pod_ids and worker_id not in runpod_pod_names:
   297→                        logger.warning(
   298→                            f"EXTERNALLY TERMINATED: Worker {worker_id} not found in RunPod"
   299→                        )
   300→                        try:
   301→                            await self._mark_worker_error(
   302→                                worker,
   303→                                "Pod externally terminated - not found in RunPod",
   304→                            )
   305→                            summary["actions"]["workers_failed"] = summary["actions"].get("workers_failed", 0) + 1
   306→                        except Exception as exc:
   307→                            logger.error(
   308→                                f"Error marking externally terminated worker {worker_id}: {exc}"
   309→                            )
   310→
   311→        except Exception as exc:
   312→            logger.error(f"Error during efficient zombie check: {exc}")
   313→
   314→
   315→__all__ = ["PeriodicChecksMixin"]
   316→
```

```
     6→

    36→async def process_api_task(task: Dict[str, Any], client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    37→    """Process API tasks by dispatching to the appropriate handler.
    38→
    39→    Args:
    40→        task: Task dictionary containing task_type and params
    41→        client: httpx client for making HTTP requests
    42→
    43→    Returns:
    44→        Result dictionary from the handler
    45→
    46→    Raises:
    47→        Exception: If task type is unsupported or handler fails
    48→    """
    49→    params = task.get("params") or {}
    50→    task_type = task.get("task_type", "unknown")
    51→
    52→    # Parse params if it's a JSON string
    53→    if isinstance(params, str):
    54→        try:
    55→            params = json.loads(params)
    56→        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
    57→            logger.error(f"Failed to parse params JSON: {e}")
    58→            raise ValueError(f"Invalid JSON in params field: {e}") from e
    59→
    60→    # Check for wavespeed api_type override (legacy support)
    61→    if task_type == "qwen_image_edit" or params.get("api_type") == "wavespeed":
    62→        task_type = "qwen_image_edit"
    63→
    64→    # Look up the handler for this task type
    65→    handler = TASK_HANDLERS.get(task_type)
    66→
    67→    if handler is None:
    68→        raise ValueError(
    69→            f"Unsupported task type: {task_type}. "
    70→            f"Supported types: {', '.join(repr(t) for t in SUPPORTED_TASK_TYPES)}."
    71→        )
    72→
    73→    # Dispatch to the handler
    74→    return await handler(task, params, client)
    75→

    77→def validate_api_environment():
    78→    """Validate all required environment variables for API orchestrator."""
    79→    logger.info("🔍 [STARTUP] Validating API orchestrator environment...")

    81→    # Required environment variables

    88→
    89→    # Optional but important environment variables

    96→    }
    97→
    98→    # Check required variables
    99→    missing_required = []
   100→    for var, description in required_vars.items():
   101→        value = os.getenv(var)
   102→        if not value:
   103→            missing_required.append(f"  ❌ {var}: {description}")

   105→        else:

   109→            else:
   110→                display_value = value
   111→            logger.info(f"[STARTUP]   ✅ {var}: {display_value}")
   112→
   113→    # Check important variables
   114→    for var, description in important_vars.items():
   115→        value = os.getenv(var)
   116→        if not value:
   117→            logger.warning(f"[STARTUP]   ⚠️  {var}: {description} (using default)")
   118→        else:
   119→            logger.info(f"[STARTUP]   ✅ {var}: {value}")
   120→
   121→    # Report results
   122→    if missing_required:
   123→        logger.error("[STARTUP] ❌ CRITICAL: Missing required environment variables:")
   124→        for msg in missing_required:
   125→            logger.error(f"[STARTUP] {msg}")
   126→        logger.error("[STARTUP] 🛑 Cannot start API orchestrator without required configuration!")
   127→        return False
   128→
   129→    logger.info("[STARTUP] ✅ Environment validation completed successfully")
   130→    return True

   132→
   133→async def main_async() -> None:
   134→    _load_runtime_environment()
   135→    setup_logging()
   136→
   137→    logger.info("[STARTUP] API Orchestrator starting...")
   138→
   139→    # Validate environment before proceeding
   140→    if not validate_api_environment():
   141→        raise RuntimeError("Missing required configuration for API orchestrator startup")
   142→
   143→    # Initialize database client for centralized logging
   144→    db_client = None
   145→    try:
   146→        db_client = DatabaseClient()
   147→
   148→        # Re-initialize logging with database client if DB logging is enabled
   149→        setup_logging(db_client=db_client, source_type="orchestrator_api")
   150→
   151→        logger.info("[STARTUP] ✅ Centralized database logging initialized")
   152→    except Exception as e:
   153→        logger.warning(f"[STARTUP] ⚠️  Could not initialize database logging: {e}")
   154→        logger.warning("[STARTUP] Continuing with console logging only...")
   155→
   156→    # Register API worker in the database (required for task claiming)
   157→    worker_id = os.getenv("API_WORKER_ID", "api-worker-main")
   158→    if db_client:
   159→        if not db_client.register_worker(worker_id):
   160→            raise RuntimeError(
   161→                f"Failed to register worker {worker_id} in database (foreign key prerequisite)"
   162→            )
   163→        logger.info(f"[STARTUP] ✅ Worker {worker_id} registered in database")
   164→    else:
   165→        logger.warning(f"[STARTUP] ⚠️  No database client - worker {worker_id} not registered")
   166→
   167→    # Log additional startup configuration
   168→    concurrency = int(os.getenv("API_WORKER_CONCURRENCY", "20"))
   169→    parent_poll_sec = int(os.getenv("API_PARENT_POLL_SEC", "10"))
   170→
   171→    logger.info(f"[STARTUP] CONCURRENCY: {concurrency}")
   172→    logger.info(f"[STARTUP] RUN_TYPE: {RUN_TYPE}")
   173→    logger.info(f"[STARTUP] PARENT_POLL_SEC: {parent_poll_sec}")
   174→    limits = httpx.Limits(
   175→        max_connections=max(64, concurrency * 4),
   176→        max_keepalive_connections=max(32, concurrency * 2),
   177→    )
   178→    active_tasks: set[asyncio.Task] = set()
   179→
   180→    async def spawn_task(task_payload: Dict[str, Any], client: httpx.AsyncClient):
   181→        task_id = task_payload.get("task_id") or task_payload.get("id")
   182→
   183→        # Set task context for logging
   184→        set_current_task(str(task_id))
   185→
   186→        try:
   187→            logger.info(f"Starting task {task_id}")
   188→            result = await process_api_task(task_payload, client)
   189→
   190→            # Check if task completion was already handled by the upload process
   191→            if result.get("_task_completed_by_upload"):
   192→                logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completion already handled by upload process, skipping additional mark_complete call")
   193→            else:
   194→                # Only call mark_complete if the upload process didn't handle it
   195→                success = await mark_complete(client, task_id, result)
   196→                if not success:
   197→                    # If we can't mark the task complete, treat it as a failure to prevent stuck tasks
   198→                    logger.error(f"Task {task_id} processed successfully but failed to save to database")
   199→                    await mark_failed(client, task_id, "Task processed successfully but failed to save completion status to database")
   200→                else:
   201→                    logger.info(f"Task {task_id} completed successfully")
   202→        except Exception as exc:
   203→            logger.error(f"Task {task_id} failed with exception: {exc}")
   204→            logger.error(f"Exception type: {type(exc).__name__}")
   205→            import traceback
   206→            logger.error(f"Full traceback: {traceback.format_exc()}")
   207→            failed_success = await mark_failed(client, task_id, str(exc))
   208→            if not failed_success:
   209→                logger.error(f"DOUBLE FAILURE: Task {task_id} failed AND could not mark as failed in database!")
   210→        finally:
   211→            # Clear task context
   212→            set_current_task(None)
   213→
   214→    async with httpx.AsyncClient(limits=limits, timeout=20.0) as client:
   215→        logger.info(
   216→            f"[WORKER_LOOP] Starting worker loop for {worker_id} with RUN_TYPE: {RUN_TYPE}, "
   217→            f"CONCURRENCY: {concurrency}, POLL_SEC: {parent_poll_sec}"
   218→        )
   219→
   220→        loop_count = 0
   221→        while True:
   222→            loop_count += 1
   223→
   224→            # prune finished subtasks
   225→            done = {t for t in active_tasks if t.done()}
   226→            if done:
   227→                logger.debug(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Pruned {len(done)} completed tasks")
   228→                active_tasks -= done
   229→
   230→            capacity = max(0, concurrency - len(active_tasks))
   231→
   232→            # Log every 10 loops or when there's activity
   233→            if loop_count % 10 == 1 or capacity > 0 or len(active_tasks) > 0:
   234→                logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Loop #{loop_count}: Active tasks: {len(active_tasks)}, Capacity: {capacity}")
   235→
   236→            # Log database logging stats periodically (every 100 loops)
   237→            if loop_count % 100 == 0:
   238→                db_stats = get_db_logging_stats()
   239→                if db_stats:
   240→                    logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] 📊 Database logging stats: {db_stats}")
   241→
   242→            if capacity > 0:
   243→                logger.debug("[WORKER_LOOP] Checking for available tasks...")
   244→                count_info = await count_tasks(client, RUN_TYPE)
   245→                available_tasks = int(count_info.get("queued_plus_active") or 0)
   246→                to_claim = min(capacity, available_tasks)
   247→
   248→                logger.debug(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Task count result: {count_info}")
   249→                logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Available tasks: {available_tasks}, Will attempt to claim: {to_claim}")
   250→
   251→                if to_claim > 0:
   252→                    logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Claiming {to_claim} tasks (capacity: {capacity}, available: {available_tasks})")
   253→
   254→                    claimed_count = 0
   255→                    for i in range(to_claim):
   256→                        logger.debug(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Attempting to claim task {i+1}/{to_claim}")
   257→                        claimed = await claim_next_task(client, worker_id, RUN_TYPE)
   258→                        if not claimed:
   259→                            logger.warning(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Failed to claim task {i+1}/{to_claim} - no tasks available despite count showing {available_tasks}")
   260→                            break
   261→                        claimed_count += 1
   262→                        logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Successfully claimed task {i+1}/{to_claim}: {claimed.get('task_id')}")
   263→                        t = asyncio.create_task(spawn_task(claimed, client))
   264→                        active_tasks.add(t)
   265→
   266→                    if claimed_count > 0:
   267→                        logger.info(f"[WORKER_LOOP] Spawned {claimed_count} tasks")
   268→                    elif available_tasks > 0:
   269→                        logger.error(f"[WORKER_LOOP] CRITICAL: Could not claim any tasks despite {available_tasks} being available - check task filters and dependencies")
   270→                elif available_tasks == 0:
   271→                    logger.debug("[WORKER_LOOP] No tasks available to claim")
   272→                else:
   273→                    logger.debug("[WORKER_LOOP] No capacity to claim tasks")
   274→
   275→            await asyncio.sleep(parent_poll_sec)
   276→
   277→
   278→def main() -> None:
   279→    try:
   280→        asyncio.run(main_async())
   281→    except RuntimeError as exc:
   282→        logger.error(f"[STARTUP] {exc}")
   283→        raise SystemExit(1) from exc
   284→
   285→
   286→if __name__ == "__main__":
   287→    main()
```

```
1→"""
     2→Task management utilities for the API orchestrator.
     3→Handles task claiming, completion, status updates, and task counting.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import os
     7→import asyncio
     8→import logging
     9→from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
    10→
    11→import httpx
    12→from supabase import create_client
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→_MISSING_ENV_LOGGED_ATTR = "_task_utils_missing_env_logged"
    16→
    17→
    18→def _get_supabase_edge_urls() -> Dict[str, str]:
    19→    """Get Supabase edge function URLs for task operations."""
    20→    base_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "").rstrip("/")
    21→    return {
    22→        "claim": f"{base_url}/functions/v1/claim-next-task" if base_url else "",
    23→        "complete": f"{base_url}/functions/v1/complete_task" if base_url else "",
    24→        "fail": f"{base_url}/functions/v1/mark-task-failed" if base_url else "",
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _auth_headers() -> Dict[str, str]:
    29→    """Get authentication headers for Supabase requests."""
    30→    token = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") or os.getenv("SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN")
    31→    return {
    32→        "Authorization": f"Bearer {token}" if token else "",
    33→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
    34→    }
    35→
    36→
    37→def _has_logged_missing_env() -> bool:
    38→    return bool(getattr(logger, _MISSING_ENV_LOGGED_ATTR, False))
    39→
    40→
    41→def _set_logged_missing_env() -> None:
    42→    setattr(logger, _MISSING_ENV_LOGGED_ATTR, True)
    43→
    44→
    45→async def count_tasks(client: httpx.AsyncClient, run_type: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    46→    """Count tasks using the new task-counts endpoint."""
    47→    try:
    48→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "").rstrip("/")
    49→        task_counts_url = f"{supabase_url}/functions/v1/task-counts"
    50→        
    51→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] Counting tasks with run_type: {run_type}")
    52→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] URL: {task_counts_url}")
    53→        
    54→        if not supabase_url:
    55→            logger.error("[TASK_COUNT] SUPABASE_URL not configured")
    56→            return {"queued_plus_active": 0, "raw": None}
    57→        
    58→        headers = _auth_headers()
    59→        if not headers["Authorization"]:
    60→            logger.error("[TASK_COUNT] Missing required request context")
    61→            return {"queued_plus_active": 0, "raw": None}
    62→
    63→        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {"include_active": False}
    64→        if run_type:
    65→            payload["run_type"] = run_type
    66→
    67→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] Sending request with payload: {payload}")
    68→
    69→        resp = await client.post(task_counts_url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=15)
    70→        
    71→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] Response status: {resp.status_code}")
    72→        
    73→        if resp.status_code != 200:
    74→            logger.warning(f"[TASK_COUNT] Task counts failed with status {resp.status_code}")
    75→            logger.error(f"[TASK_COUNT] Error response: {resp.text}")
    76→            return {"queued_plus_active": 0, "raw": None}
    77→            
    78→        data = resp.json()
    79→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] Raw response data: {data}")
    80→        
    81→        # Extract the counts from the new endpoint format
    82→        # Assuming it returns the same structure as the old endpoint
    83→        if "totals" in data:
    84→            totals = data["totals"]
    85→            # For API workers, only consider queued tasks as claimable
    86→            available_count = int(totals.get("queued_only", totals.get("queued_plus_active", 0)))
    87→            logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] Using totals.queued_only: {available_count}")
    88→        else:
    89→            # Fallback: look for common count fields
    90→            available_count = int(data.get("queued_plus_active", data.get("available_tasks", 0)))
    91→            logger.debug(f"[TASK_COUNT] Using fallback count: {available_count}")
    92→        
    93→        logger.info(f"[TASK_COUNT] Available tasks: {available_count}")
    94→        return {"queued_plus_active": available_count, "raw": data}
    95→        
    96→    except (httpx.HTTPError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
    97→        logger.error(f"Count tasks failed: {exc}")
    98→        return {"queued_plus_active": 0, "raw": None}
    99→
   100→
   101→async def claim_next_task(client: httpx.AsyncClient, worker_id: str, run_type: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   102→    """Claim the next available task for processing."""
   103→    urls = _get_supabase_edge_urls()
   104→    headers = _auth_headers()
   105→    
   106→    # Enhanced logging for debugging
   107→    logger.debug(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Worker {worker_id} attempting to claim task with run_type: {run_type}")
   108→    logger.debug(f"[TASK_CLAIM] URLs: {urls}")
   109→    
   110→    if not urls["claim"] or not headers["Authorization"]:
   111→        if not _has_logged_missing_env():
   112→            logger.error("[TASK_CLAIM] Missing endpoint configuration or request context; cannot claim tasks")
   113→            _set_logged_missing_env()
   114→        await asyncio.sleep(1)
   115→        return None
   116→
   117→    payload: Dict[str, Any] = {"worker_id": worker_id}
   118→    if run_type:
   119→        payload["run_type"] = run_type
   120→    
   121→    logger.debug(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Sending claim request with payload: {payload}")
   122→    
   123→    try:
   124→        resp = await client.post(urls["claim"], headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=15)
   125→        
   126→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Response status: {resp.status_code}")
   127→        
   128→        if resp.status_code == 204:
   129→            logger.info(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Worker {worker_id}: No tasks available to claim (204)")
   130→            return None
   131→            
   132→        resp.raise_for_status()
   133→        data = resp.json()
   134→        logger.info(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Worker {worker_id}: Successfully claimed task: {data.get('task_id')} ({data.get('task_type')})")
   135→        logger.debug(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Full task data: {data}")
   136→        
   137→        # No task type filtering - process any task with matching run_type
   138→        # The run_type filtering is handled by the edge function
   139→            
   140→        return data
   141→    except (httpx.HTTPError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   142→        logger.error(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Worker {worker_id}: Claim failed with error: {exc}")
   143→        if isinstance(exc, httpx.HTTPStatusError):
   144→            logger.error(f"[TASK_CLAIM] Response body: {exc.response.text}")
   145→        await asyncio.sleep(0.5)
   146→        return None
   147→
   148→
   149→async def mark_complete_via_edge_function(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, output_location: str = None) -> bool:
   150→    """Mark task complete via edge function.
   151→
   152→    Uses complete_task when no output_location is provided (file upload handled separately),
   153→    or update-task-status when we only have an output_location URL (e.g. fallback to external URL
   154→    after upload failure). The complete_task endpoint requires file_data or storage_path, so
   155→    passing just an output_location would fail with 400.
   156→    """
   157→    try:
   158→        headers = _auth_headers()
   159→
   160→        if not headers["Authorization"]:
   161→            logger.error("Missing request context for mark-task-complete")
   162→            return False
   163→
   164→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "").rstrip("/")
   165→
   166→        if output_location:
   167→            # Use update-task-status for URL-only completion (complete_task requires file data)
   168→            edge_url = f"{supabase_url}/functions/v1/update-task-status" if supabase_url else ""
   169→            payload = {
   170→                "task_id": task_id,
   171→                "status": "Completed",
   172→                "output_location": output_location
   173→            }
   174→        else:
   175→            urls = _get_supabase_edge_urls()
   176→            edge_url = urls["complete"]
   177→            payload = {"task_id": task_id}
   178→
   179→        if not edge_url:
   180→            logger.error("Missing edge function URL configuration")
   181→            return False
   182→
   183→        logger.info(f"Attempting to mark task {task_id} complete via {edge_url}")
   184→        logger.debug(f"Payload: {payload}")
   185→
   186→        resp = await client.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=30)
   187→
   188→        # Log response details for debugging
   189→        logger.info(f"Mark complete response: status={resp.status_code}, headers={dict(resp.headers)}")
   190→        if resp.status_code != 200:
   191→            response_text = resp.text
   192→            logger.error(f"Mark complete failed with status {resp.status_code}: {response_text}")
   193→
   194→        resp.raise_for_status()
   195→
   196→        # Log response body for successful requests too
   197→        try:
   198→            response_json = resp.json()
   199→            logger.info(f"Task {task_id} marked complete successfully: {response_json}")
   200→        except ValueError:
   201→            logger.info(f"Task {task_id} marked complete (no JSON response)")
   202→
   203→        return True
   204→
   205→    except (httpx.HTTPError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   206→        logger.error(f"Failed to mark task {task_id} complete: {exc}")
   207→        return False
   208→
   209→
   210→async def mark_failed_via_edge_function(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, error_message: str) -> bool:
   211→    """Mark task failed via update-task-status edge function"""
   212→    try:
   213→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL", "").rstrip("/")
   214→        edge_url = (
   215→            os.getenv("SUPABASE_EDGE_UPDATE_TASK_URL")
   216→            or (f"{supabase_url}/functions/v1/update-task-status" if supabase_url else None)
   217→        )
   218→        
   219→        if not edge_url:
   220→            logger.error("No update-task-status edge URL configured")
   221→            return False
   222→            
   223→        headers = {
   224→            "Content-Type": "application/json", 
   225→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {os.getenv('SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN') or os.getenv('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')}"
   226→        }
   227→        
   228→        payload = {
   229→            "task_id": task_id,
   230→            "status": "Failed",
   231→            "output_location": error_message[:500]  # Truncate long error messages
   232→        }
   233→        
   234→        logger.info(f"Attempting to mark task {task_id} failed via {edge_url}")
   235→        logger.debug(f"Payload: {payload}")
   236→        
   237→        resp = await client.post(edge_url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=30)
   238→        
   239→        # Log response details for debugging
   240→        logger.info(f"Mark failed response: status={resp.status_code}, headers={dict(resp.headers)}")
   241→        if resp.status_code != 200:
   242→            response_text = resp.text
   243→            logger.error(f"Mark failed failed with status {resp.status_code}: {response_text}")
   244→            
   245→        resp.raise_for_status()
   246→        
   247→        # Log response body for successful requests too
   248→        try:
   249→            response_json = resp.json()
   250→            logger.info(f"Task {task_id} marked failed successfully: {response_json}")
   251→        except ValueError:
   252→            logger.info(f"Task {task_id} marked failed (no JSON response)")
   253→            
   254→        return True
   255→        
   256→    except (httpx.HTTPError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   257→        logger.error(f"Failed to mark task {task_id} as failed: {exc}")
   258→        return False
   259→
   260→
   261→async def mark_complete(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   262→    """Mark task complete - wrapper for backward compatibility"""
   263→    output_location = result.get("output_location")
   264→    success = await mark_complete_via_edge_function(client, task_id, output_location)
   265→    if not success:
   266→        logger.error(f"CRITICAL: Failed to mark task {task_id} as complete in database - task may remain stuck!")
   267→        logger.error(f"Task result that failed to save: {result}")
   268→    return success
   269→
   270→
   271→async def mark_failed(client: httpx.AsyncClient, task_id: str, error_message: str) -> bool:
   272→    """Mark task failed - wrapper for backward compatibility"""
   273→    success = await mark_failed_via_edge_function(client, task_id, error_message)
   274→    if not success:
   275→        logger.error(f"CRITICAL: Failed to mark task {task_id} as failed in database - task may remain stuck!")
   276→        logger.error(f"Error message that failed to save: {error_message}")
   277→    return success
   278→
   279→
   280→async def update_task_metadata(task_id: str, metadata_updates: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   281→    """
   282→    Update task metadata (result_data field) to store tracking information.
   283→    
   284→    This is used to store fal.ai request IDs and other tracking data
   285→    that allows recovery if the orchestrator crashes mid-job.
   286→    
   287→    Args:
   288→        task_id: The task ID to update
   289→        metadata_updates: Dictionary of key-value pairs to merge into result_data
   290→        
   291→    Returns:
   292→        True if successful, False otherwise
   293→    """
   294→    try:
   295→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL")
   296→        supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
   297→        
   298→        if not supabase_url or not supabase_key:
   299→            logger.error("Missing SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY for metadata update")
   300→            return False
   301→        
   302→        client = create_client(supabase_url, supabase_key)
   303→        
   304→        # First, get the current result_data to merge with
   305→        result = client.table('tasks').select('result_data').eq('id', task_id).execute()
   306→        
   307→        if not result.data:
   308→            logger.error(f"Task {task_id} not found for metadata update")
   309→            return False
   310→        
   311→        current_data = result.data[0].get('result_data') or {}
   312→        
   313→        # Merge the new metadata
   314→        updated_data = {**current_data, **metadata_updates}
   315→        
   316→        # Update the task
   317→        update_result = client.table('tasks').update({
   318→            'result_data': updated_data
   319→        }).eq('id', task_id).execute()
   320→        
   321→        if update_result.data:
   322→            logger.info(f"Updated task {task_id} metadata with: {list(metadata_updates.keys())}")
   323→            return True
   324→        else:
   325→            logger.error(f"Failed to update task {task_id} metadata - no data returned")
   326→            return False
   327→            
   328→    except Exception as exc:
   329→        logger.exception(f"Failed to update task {task_id} metadata: {exc}")
   330→        return False
   331→
   332→
   333→async def get_task_metadata(task_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   334→    """
   335→    Get task metadata (result_data field) for recovery purposes.
   336→    
   337→    Args:
   338→        task_id: The task ID to query
   339→        
   340→    Returns:
   341→        The result_data dict if found, None otherwise
   342→    """
   343→    try:
   344→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL")
   345→        supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
   346→        
   347→        if not supabase_url or not supabase_key:
   348→            logger.error("Missing SUPABASE_URL or SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY for metadata query")
   349→            return None
   350→        
   351→        client = create_client(supabase_url, supabase_key)
   352→        
   353→        result = client.table('tasks').select('result_data').eq('id', task_id).execute()
   354→        
   355→        if result.data:
   356→            return result.data[0].get('result_data') or {}
   357→        else:
   358→            logger.warning(f"Task {task_id} not found for metadata query")
   359→            return None
   360→            
   361→    except Exception as exc:
   362→        logger.error(f"Failed to get task {task_id} metadata: {exc}")
   363→        return None
   364→
```

```
    81→    async def run_single_cycle(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    82→        """Run a single orchestrator cycle and return summary of actions taken."""
    83→        cycle_start = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    84→        self.cycle_count += 1
    85→
    86→        try:
    87→            from .logging_config import set_current_cycle
    88→
    89→            set_current_cycle(self.cycle_count)
    90→        except Exception:
    91→            pass
    92→
    93→        print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    94→        print(f"ORCHESTRATOR CYCLE #{self.cycle_count}")
    95→        print("=" * 80 + "\n")
    96→
    97→        logger.info("Starting orchestrator cycle")
    98→
    99→        summary = CycleSummary()
   100→
   101→        try:
   102→            workers, task_counts, detailed_counts = await self._fetch_current_state()
   103→
   104→            worker_states = await self._derive_all_worker_states(workers, task_counts.queued)
   105→
   106→            worker_states = await self._handle_early_termination(worker_states, task_counts, summary)
   107→
   108→            await self._handle_spawning_workers(worker_states, task_counts, summary)
   109→
   110→            await self._health_check_active_workers(worker_states, task_counts, summary)
   111→
   112→            await self._cleanup_error_workers(worker_states, summary)
   113→
   114→            await self._reconcile_orphaned_tasks(worker_states, summary)
   115→
   116→            scaling = await self._calculate_scaling_decision(worker_states, task_counts)
   117→
   118→            await self._execute_scaling(scaling, worker_states, task_counts, summary, detailed_counts)
   119→
   120→            await self._run_periodic_checks(worker_states, summary)
   121→
   122→        except Exception as exc:
   123→            logger.error(f"Error in orchestrator cycle: {exc}")
   124→            summary.error = str(exc)
   125→
   126→        cycle_duration = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - cycle_start).total_seconds()
   127→        logger.info(f"Orchestrator cycle completed in {cycle_duration:.2f}s: {summary.to_dict()['actions']}")
   128→
   129→        total_workers = len([ws for ws in worker_states if not ws.is_terminal]) if "worker_states" in dir() else 0
   130→        self.health_monitor.check_scaling_anomaly(
   131→            task_count=task_counts.total if "task_counts" in dir() else 0,
   132→            worker_count=total_workers,
   133→            workers_spawned=summary.workers_spawned,
   134→        )
   135→        self.health_monitor.log_health_summary(self.cycle_count)
   136→
   137→        if self.cycle_count % 10 == 0:
   138→            if not self.health_monitor.check_logging_health():
   139→                logger.error("Database logging is degraded - check logs above")
   140→
   141→        return {
   142→            "timestamp": cycle_start.isoformat(),
   143→            **summary.to_dict(),
   144→        }
   145→
```

```
1→"""Worker lifecycle management phases (spawning, early termination, cleanup, reconciliation)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import math
     7→from datetime import datetime, timezone
     8→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Dict, List
     9→
    10→from gpu_orchestrator.worker_state import CycleSummary, DerivedWorkerState, TaskCounts, WorkerLifecycle
    11→
    12→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    15→    from gpu_orchestrator.control.contracts import ControlHost
    16→
    17→
    18→class LifecyclePhaseMixin:
    19→    """Lifecycle phase methods not directly tied to active-health probing."""
    20→
    21→    async def _handle_early_termination(
    22→        self: "ControlHost",
    23→        worker_states: List[DerivedWorkerState],
    24→        task_counts: TaskCounts,
    25→        summary: CycleSummary,
    26→    ) -> List[DerivedWorkerState]:
    27→        """
    28→        Terminate excess spawning workers before they finish starting up.
    29→        Returns updated worker_states list with terminated workers removed.
    30→        """
    31→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    32→        config = self.config
    33→
    34→        spawning_states = [ws for ws in worker_states if ws.is_spawning]
    35→        active_states = [ws for ws in worker_states if ws.is_active and not ws.is_terminal]
    36→
    37→        current_capacity = len(active_states) + len(spawning_states)
    38→
    39→        if task_counts.total > 0:
    40→            down_scaled = math.ceil(task_counts.total * config.scale_down_multiplier)
    41→            task_based = max(1, down_scaled)
    42→        else:
    43→            task_based = 0
    44→
    45→        early_desired = max(config.min_active_gpus, task_based, config.machines_to_keep_idle)
    46→        early_desired = min(early_desired, config.max_active_gpus)
    47→
    48→        if current_capacity <= early_desired:
    49→            return worker_states
    50→
    51→        if task_counts.queued > 0:
    52→            logger.debug("Skipping early termination because queued work exists")
    53→            return worker_states
    54→
    55→        time_since_last_down = None
    56→        if self.last_scale_down_at:
    57→            time_since_last_down = (now - self.last_scale_down_at).total_seconds()
    58→
    59→        if (
    60→            time_since_last_down is not None
    61→            and time_since_last_down < config.scale_down_grace_period_sec
    62→        ):
    63→            logger.debug(
    64→                "Skipping early termination due to scale-down grace period: "
    65→                f"{time_since_last_down:.0f}s < {config.scale_down_grace_period_sec}s"
    66→            )
    67→            return worker_states
    68→
    69→        excess = current_capacity - early_desired
    70→        eligible: List[DerivedWorkerState] = []
    71→        for ws in sorted(spawning_states, key=lambda x: x.created_at, reverse=True):
    72→            age = (now - ws.created_at).total_seconds()
    73→            if age > config.spawning_grace_period_sec:
    74→                eligible.append(ws)
    75→
    76→        to_terminate = min(excess, len(eligible))
    77→
    78→        if to_terminate == 0:
    79→            logger.debug("No spawning workers eligible for early termination (within grace period)")
    80→            return worker_states
    81→
    82→        logger.info(
    83→            f"Early termination: {current_capacity} workers > {early_desired} desired, "
    84→            f"terminating {to_terminate} spawning workers"
    85→        )
    86→
    87→        terminated_ids = set()
    88→        for ws in eligible[:to_terminate]:
    89→            if ws.runpod_id:
    90→                success = self.runpod.terminate_worker(ws.runpod_id)
    91→                if success:
    92→                    logger.info(f"Terminated spawning worker {ws.worker_id} (RunPod {ws.runpod_id})")
    93→                else:
    94→                    logger.warning(f"Failed to terminate spawning worker {ws.worker_id}")
    95→
    96→            metadata_update = {
    97→                "termination_reason": f"early_termination_over_capacity ({current_capacity} > {early_desired})",
    98→                "terminated_at": now.isoformat(),
    99→            }
   100→            await self.db.update_worker_status(ws.worker_id, "terminated", metadata_update)
   101→            summary.workers_terminated += 1
   102→            terminated_ids.add(ws.worker_id)
   103→
   104→        self.last_scale_down_at = now
   105→
   106→        return [ws for ws in worker_states if ws.worker_id not in terminated_ids]
   107→
   108→    async def _handle_spawning_workers(
   109→        self: "ControlHost",
   110→        worker_states: List[DerivedWorkerState],
   111→        task_counts: TaskCounts,
   112→        summary: CycleSummary,
   113→    ) -> None:
   114→        """Handle pod initialization, script launch, and promotion."""
   115→        spawning_states = [
   116→            ws for ws in worker_states if ws.is_spawning or ws.should_promote_to_active
   117→        ]
   118→
   119→        for ws in spawning_states:
   120→            worker_id = ws.worker_id
   121→            runpod_id = ws.runpod_id
   122→
   123→            if not runpod_id:
   124→                await self._mark_worker_error_by_id(worker_id, "No Runpod ID")
   125→                summary.workers_failed += 1
   126→                continue
   127→
   128→            if ws.should_promote_to_active:
   129→                logger.info(f"Worker {worker_id} signaled ready_for_tasks - promoting to active")
   130→                metadata_update = {
   131→                    "promoted_to_active_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   132→                    "promotion_reason": "ready_for_tasks",
   133→                }
   134→                await self.db.update_worker_status(worker_id, "active", metadata_update)
   135→                summary.workers_promoted += 1
   136→                continue
   137→
   138→            status_update = self.runpod.check_and_initialize_worker(worker_id, runpod_id)
   139→
   140→            if status_update["status"] == "error":
   141→                await self._mark_worker_error_by_id(worker_id, status_update.get("error", "Unknown error"))
   142→                summary.workers_failed += 1
   143→                continue
   144→
   145→            if ws.should_terminate:
   146→                await self._mark_worker_error_by_id(worker_id, ws.termination_reason or "Spawning timeout")
   147→                summary.workers_failed += 1
   148→                continue
   149→
   150→            if status_update.get("ready") and status_update["status"] == "spawning":
   151→                if ws.lifecycle == WorkerLifecycle.SPAWNING_SCRIPT_PENDING:
   152→                    if self.config.auto_start_worker_process:
   153→                        if self.runpod.start_worker_process(
   154→                            runpod_id,
   155→                            worker_id,
   156→                            has_pending_tasks=(task_counts.queued > 0),
   157→                        ):
   158→                            logger.info(f"Launched startup script for {worker_id}")
   159→                            metadata_update = {
   160→                                "ssh_details": status_update.get("ssh_details"),
   161→                                "startup_script_launched": True,
   162→                                "startup_script_launched_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   163→                            }
   164→                            await self.db.update_worker_status(worker_id, "spawning", metadata_update)
   165→                            summary.startup_scripts_launched += 1
   166→                        else:
   167→                            logger.warning(f"Failed to launch startup script for {worker_id}")
   168→
   169→    async def _cleanup_error_workers(
   170→        self: "ControlHost",
   171→        worker_states: List[DerivedWorkerState],
   172→        summary: CycleSummary,
   173→    ) -> None:
   174→        """Terminate RunPod instances for error workers."""
   175→        error_states = [ws for ws in worker_states if ws.lifecycle == WorkerLifecycle.ERROR]
   176→
   177→        for ws in error_states:
   178→            try:
   179→                worker = await self.db.get_worker_by_id(ws.worker_id)
   180→                if worker:
   181→                    await self._check_error_worker_cleanup(worker)
   182→                    summary.workers_failed += 1
   183→            except Exception as exc:
   184→                logger.error(f"Error during error worker cleanup for {ws.worker_id}: {exc}")
   185→
   186→    async def _reconcile_orphaned_tasks(
   187→        self: "ControlHost",
   188→        worker_states: List[DerivedWorkerState],
   189→        summary: CycleSummary,
   190→    ) -> None:
   191→        """Reset tasks stuck on failed/terminated workers."""
   192→        failed_ids = []
   193→        for ws in worker_states:
   194→            if ws.lifecycle in (WorkerLifecycle.ERROR, WorkerLifecycle.TERMINATED):
   195→                failed_ids.append(ws.worker_id)
   196→
   197→        if failed_ids:
   198→            reset_count = await self.db.reset_orphaned_tasks(failed_ids[:100])
   199→            summary.tasks_reset = reset_count
   200→
   201→        unassigned_reset = await self.db.reset_unassigned_orphaned_tasks(timeout_minutes=15)
   202→        summary.tasks_reset += unassigned_reset
   203→
   204→        stale_reset = await self.db.reset_stale_assigned_tasks(timeout_minutes=30)
   205→        summary.tasks_reset += stale_reset
   206→
   207→    async def _check_worker_task_failure_streak(self: "ControlHost", worker_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   208→        """
   209→        Check if a worker is repeatedly failing tasks.
   210→
   211→        Queries recent task outcomes to detect workers stuck in failure loops
   212→        (e.g., OOM errors causing every task to fail).
   213→        """
   214→        try:
   215→            stats = await self.db.get_worker_task_failure_streak(
   216→                worker_id,
   217→                window_minutes=self.config.task_failure_window_minutes,
   218→            )
   219→
   220→            consecutive = stats["consecutive_failures"]
   221→            threshold = self.config.max_consecutive_task_failures
   222→
   223→            if consecutive >= threshold:
   224→                reason = (
   225→                    f"Task failure loop detected: {consecutive} consecutive failures "
   226→                    f"(threshold: {threshold}). Possible OOM or worker corruption."
   227→                )
   228→                logger.warning(f"[TASK_FAILURE_STREAK] Worker {worker_id}: {reason}")
   229→                return {
   230→                    "should_restart": True,
   231→                    "consecutive_failures": consecutive,
   232→                    "total_failures": stats["total_failures"],
   233→                    "total_outcomes": stats["total_outcomes"],
   234→                    "failure_rate": stats["failure_rate"],
   235→                    "reason": reason,
   236→                }
   237→
   238→            if consecutive > 0:
   239→                logger.debug(
   240→                    f"[TASK_FAILURE_STREAK] Worker {worker_id}: {consecutive} consecutive failures "
   241→                    f"(threshold: {threshold}, rate: {stats['failure_rate']:.0%})"
   242→                )
   243→
   244→            return {
   245→                "should_restart": False,
   246→                "consecutive_failures": consecutive,
   247→                "total_failures": stats["total_failures"],
   248→                "total_outcomes": stats["total_outcomes"],
   249→                "failure_rate": stats["failure_rate"],
   250→                "reason": None,
```

## Summary of Stuck/Stale "In Progress" Task Detection and Recovery Mechanisms

Based on my thorough search of the Headless WGP Orchestrator codebase, here are **all mechanisms that detect and recover stuck/stale "In Progress" tasks**:

### **1. PRIMARY TASK RECOVERY MECHANISM: `_reconcile_orphaned_tasks()` (GPU Orchestrator)**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/control/phases/lifecycle.py` (lines 186-205)

**Execution:** Runs every orchestrator control loop cycle (default 30-second intervals)

**Contains THREE sub-mechanisms:**

#### A. **Reset Tasks from Failed Workers** - `reset_orphaned_tasks()`
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py` (lines 378-428)
- **Trigger:** When worker is marked as ERROR or TERMINATED
- **Logic:**
  - Finds tasks in "In Progress" status assigned to failed worker IDs
  - Only resets tasks with `attempts < 3` (allows retries)
  - Sets task status back to "Queued"
  - Clears `worker_id`, `generation_started_at`, `generation_processed_at`
  - Sets error_message: "Reset - orphaned from failed worker"

#### B. **Reset Unassigned Orphaned Tasks** - `reset_unassigned_orphaned_tasks(timeout_minutes=15)`
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py` (lines 430-474)
- **Timeout Configuration:** 15 minutes (hardcoded)
- **Trigger:** Every cycle through `_reconcile_orphaned_tasks()` (line 201 of lifecycle.py)
- **Criteria:**
  - Status = "In Progress"
  - `worker_id` IS NULL (no assigned worker)
  - `generation_started_at` is older than 15 minutes
  - `attempts < 3`
  - **Explicitly excludes orchestrator tasks** (those with '_orchestrator' in task_type)
- **Error message:** "Reset - stuck in progress with no worker assigned"

#### C. **Reset Stale Assigned Tasks** - `reset_stale_assigned_tasks(timeout_minutes=30)`
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py` (lines 531-594)
- **Timeout Configuration:** 30 minutes (hardcoded, based on `updated_at` not `generation_started_at`)
- **Trigger:** Every cycle through `_reconcile_orphaned_tasks()` (line 204 of lifecycle.py)
- **Criteria:**
  - Status = "In Progress"
  - Has worker_id assigned (not api-worker-*)
  - `updated_at` hasn't changed for 30+ minutes (catching stalled tasks)
  - `attempts < 3`
  - **Explicitly excludes orchestrator tasks**
- **Use Case:** Safety net for tasks where worker is alive/heartbeating but task processing itself is stuck (deadlock, infinite loop)
- **Error message:** "Reset - no activity for 30 minutes (stale task)"

### **2. SECONDARY TASK RECOVERY: Stuck Task Detection via Active Worker Health Checks**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/control/phases/health.py` (lines 229-259)

**Method:** `_check_stuck_tasks()`

**Timeout Configuration:**
- **File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/config.py` (line 111)
- **Default:** `TASK_STUCK_TIMEOUT_SEC = 1200` (20 minutes)
- **Environment variable:** `TASK_STUCK_TIMEOUT_SEC`

**Logic:**
- Runs during health check of active workers (every cycle)
- Checks `generation_started_at` for running tasks
- If task has been running longer than `task_stuck_timeout_sec` (20 min default):
  - Marks the entire worker as ERROR
  - Triggers orphaned task cleanup (via `_mark_worker_error()`)
  - Terminates worker's RunPod instance

### **3. API WORKER ORPHANED TASK RECOVERY** - `reset_api_worker_orphaned_tasks(timeout_minutes=5)`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py` (lines 476-529)

**Timeout Configuration:** 5 minutes (hardcoded)

**Context:** API workers (like `api-worker-main`) are not tracked in workers table, so their in-flight tasks can become orphaned when API orchestrator crashes/restarts

**Criteria:**
- Status = "In Progress"
- `worker_id` matches pattern `api-worker-%`
- `generation_started_at` is older than 5 minutes
- `attempts < 3`

**Logic:**
- Resets tasks individually (not batch)
- Properly increments attempts counter
- Error message: "Reset - orphaned from API worker timeout (attempt N/3)"

**Note:** This method is NOT currently called in the control loop - it exists but would need to be integrated

### **4. CONFIGURATION & TIMEOUT SUMMARY**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/config.py`

| Mechanism | Timeout | Line | Env Variable | Default |
|-----------|---------|------|--------------|---------|
| Unassigned orphaned task reset | 15 min | hardcoded | None | 15 |
| Stale assigned task reset (updated_at) | 30 min | hardcoded | None | 30 |
| Stuck task detection (generation_started_at) | 20 min | 111 | `TASK_STUCK_TIMEOUT_SEC` | 1200s |
| API worker orphaned reset | 5 min | hardcoded | None | 5 |

### **5. CONTROL LOOP INTEGRATION**

**Main Orchestrator Control Loop:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/control_loop.py`

**Execution sequence (every cycle, default 30-second intervals):**

```
Line 114: await self._reconcile_orphaned_tasks(worker_states, summary)
          ├─ Reset tasks from failed workers (immediate)
          ├─ Reset unassigned tasks stuck 15+ minutes
          └─ Reset stale assigned tasks with no activity 30+ minutes
```

Also in parallel:
```
Line 110: await self._health_check_active_workers(worker_states, task_counts, summary)
          └─ Line 208: await self._check_stuck_tasks(ws, summary)
             └─ Checks generation_started_at against task_stuck_timeout_sec (20 min default)
```

### **6. KEY FILES FOR REFERENCE**

**Task Reset Operations:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py` (lines 364-594)
- Methods: `_build_task_reset_payload()`, `reset_orphaned_tasks()`, `reset_unassigned_orphaned_tasks()`, `reset_stale_assigned_tasks()`, `reset_api_worker_orphaned_tasks()`

**Control Loop Coordination:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/control_loop.py` (lines 81-144)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless_WGP_Orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/control/phases/lifecycle.py` (lines 186-205)

**Health Monitoring:**

### **7. IMPORTANT NOTES**

1. **Orchestrator task exclusion:** Both `reset_unassigned_orphaned_tasks()` and `reset_stale_assigned_tasks()` explicitly exclude tasks with `'_orchestrator'` in their `task_type` because orchestrator tasks can legitimately run for extended periods

2. **Attempts tracking:** All reset mechanisms respect the `attempts < 3` limit, allowing tasks to be retried up to 3 times before being abandoned

3. **Missing integration:** The `reset_api_worker_orphaned_tasks()` method exists (5-minute timeout for API worker tasks) but is NOT currently called in the control loop - it would need to be integrated

4. **Dual timeout approach:** The system uses two different timestamp fields:
   - `generation_started_at`: Used by stuck task detection (20 min)
   - `updated_at`: Used by stale task reset (30 min) - catches tasks that made progress but then stalled

5. **Worker failure triggers reset:** When any worker is marked as ERROR, `_mark_worker_error()` immediately calls `reset_orphaned_tasks()` to clean up that worker's tasks
